## 4. FULL_DATABASE_SCRIPT

```sql
-- REG v1 — oracle19c — copy-and-run against a clean schema
-- BLOCK 1 SEQUENCES: none — every PK uses the identity clause

-- BLOCK 2 PARENT TABLES
CREATE TABLE REG_SVC_PKG (
    SERVICE_PACKAGE_ID NUMBER(19) GENERATED BY DEFAULT AS IDENTITY NOT NULL,
    SERVICE_CODE VARCHAR2(100 CHAR) NOT NULL,
    AVAILABLE NUMBER(1) DEFAULT 1 NOT NULL,
    REGISTERED_AT TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE NOT NULL,
    WITHDRAWN_AT TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE
);

CREATE TABLE REG_CONNECTION (
    CONNECTION_ID NUMBER(19) GENERATED BY DEFAULT AS IDENTITY NOT NULL,
    CONNECTION_NAME VARCHAR2(100 CHAR) NOT NULL,
    CONNECTION_TYPE VARCHAR2(10 CHAR) NOT NULL,
    ENDPOINT VARCHAR2(500 CHAR) NOT NULL,
    QUERY_TOOL VARCHAR2(100 CHAR),
    DIALECT VARCHAR2(30 CHAR) NOT NULL,
    CREDENTIAL_REFERENCE VARCHAR2(200 CHAR) NOT NULL,
    READ_ONLY NUMBER(1) NOT NULL,
    LIMITED_TO_VIEWS NUMBER(1) DEFAULT 0 NOT NULL,
    ENVIRONMENT_NAME VARCHAR2(50 CHAR) NOT NULL,
    ACTIVATED_AT TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE NOT NULL
);

-- BLOCK 3 CHILD TABLES
CREATE TABLE REG_SVC_PKG_VER (
    SERVICE_PACKAGE_VERSION_ID NUMBER(19) GENERATED BY DEFAULT AS IDENTITY NOT NULL,
    VERSION_NUMBER NUMBER(10,0) NOT NULL,
    SERVICE_KNOWLEDGE CLOB NOT NULL,
    SERVICE_DEFINITION CLOB NOT NULL,
    INPUT_NAME VARCHAR2(100 CHAR) NOT NULL,
    FETCH_MODE VARCHAR2(10 CHAR) NOT NULL,
    DOCUMENT_SOURCE_QUERY_NAME VARCHAR2(100 CHAR),
    DOCUMENT_TYPE_COLUMN VARCHAR2(128 CHAR),
    DOCUMENT_PATH_COLUMN VARCHAR2(128 CHAR),
    DOCUMENT_CONTENT_COLUMN VARCHAR2(128 CHAR),
    APPROVAL_ENABLED NUMBER(1) DEFAULT 0 NOT NULL,
    APPROVAL_API VARCHAR2(500 CHAR),
    CONTENT_HASH VARCHAR2(64 CHAR) NOT NULL,
    LOADED_AT TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE NOT NULL,
    SERVICE_PACKAGE_ID NUMBER(19) NOT NULL
);

CREATE TABLE REG_SVC_QUERY (
    SERVICE_QUERY_ID NUMBER(19) GENERATED BY DEFAULT AS IDENTITY NOT NULL,
    QUERY_NAME VARCHAR2(100 CHAR) NOT NULL,
    CONNECTION_NAME VARCHAR2(100 CHAR) NOT NULL,
    SQL_TEXT CLOB NOT NULL,
    SERVICE_PACKAGE_VERSION_ID NUMBER(19) NOT NULL
);

CREATE TABLE REG_REQ_DOC (
    REQUIRED_DOCUMENT_ID NUMBER(19) GENERATED BY DEFAULT AS IDENTITY NOT NULL,
    DOCUMENT_TYPE VARCHAR2(100 CHAR) NOT NULL,
    SERVICE_PACKAGE_VERSION_ID NUMBER(19) NOT NULL
);

-- REG_LOAD_RESULT has no FK; created after the registry tables
CREATE TABLE REG_LOAD_RESULT (
    LOAD_RESULT_ID NUMBER(19) GENERATED BY DEFAULT AS IDENTITY NOT NULL,
    LOAD_RUN_AT TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE NOT NULL,
    SUBJECT_KIND VARCHAR2(20 CHAR) NOT NULL,
    SUBJECT_NAME VARCHAR2(200 CHAR) NOT NULL,
    SERVICE_CODE VARCHAR2(100 CHAR),
    VERSION_NUMBER NUMBER(10,0),
    OUTCOME VARCHAR2(20 CHAR) NOT NULL,
    REASON VARCHAR2(1000 CHAR),
    CREATED_AT TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE DEFAULT SYSTIMESTAMP NOT NULL,
    UPDATED_AT TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE DEFAULT SYSTIMESTAMP NOT NULL
);

-- BLOCK 4 COMMENTS
COMMENT ON TABLE REG_SVC_PKG IS 'ENT-REG-001 Service Package — one row per service code';
COMMENT ON COLUMN REG_SVC_PKG.SERVICE_PACKAGE_ID IS 'DBF-REG-001 ENT-REG-001.servicePackageId — Identifier (identity)';
COMMENT ON COLUMN REG_SVC_PKG.SERVICE_CODE IS 'DBF-REG-002 ENT-REG-001.serviceCode — Service code — unique, immutable, canonical lower case (SERVICE_CODE; ADR-REG-017)';
COMMENT ON COLUMN REG_SVC_PKG.AVAILABLE IS 'DBF-REG-003 ENT-REG-001.available — 1 = available, 0 = withdrawn';
COMMENT ON COLUMN REG_SVC_PKG.REGISTERED_AT IS 'DBF-REG-004 ENT-REG-001.registeredAt — First load of the service code';
COMMENT ON COLUMN REG_SVC_PKG.WITHDRAWN_AT IS 'DBF-REG-005 ENT-REG-001.withdrawnAt — Set when withdrawn';
COMMENT ON TABLE REG_SVC_PKG_VER IS 'ENT-REG-002 Service Package Version — immutable, never deleted (ADR-REG-003)';
COMMENT ON COLUMN REG_SVC_PKG_VER.SERVICE_PACKAGE_VERSION_ID IS 'DBF-REG-006 ENT-REG-002.servicePackageVersionId — Identifier (identity)';
COMMENT ON COLUMN REG_SVC_PKG_VER.VERSION_NUMBER IS 'DBF-REG-007 ENT-REG-002.versionNumber — Version number — unique per service package';
COMMENT ON COLUMN REG_SVC_PKG_VER.SERVICE_KNOWLEDGE IS 'DBF-REG-008 ENT-REG-002.serviceKnowledge — Service knowledge — whole text, unaltered';
COMMENT ON COLUMN REG_SVC_PKG_VER.SERVICE_DEFINITION IS 'DBF-REG-009 ENT-REG-002.serviceDefinition — Service definition — as loaded';
COMMENT ON COLUMN REG_SVC_PKG_VER.INPUT_NAME IS 'DBF-REG-010 ENT-REG-002.inputName — Name of the only bind parameter';
COMMENT ON COLUMN REG_SVC_PKG_VER.FETCH_MODE IS 'DBF-REG-011 ENT-REG-002.fetchMode — Fetch mode: path | blob | manual (FETCH_MODE)';
COMMENT ON COLUMN REG_SVC_PKG_VER.DOCUMENT_SOURCE_QUERY_NAME IS 'DBF-REG-012 ENT-REG-002.documentSourceQueryName — Query that lists the documents (path / blob)';
COMMENT ON COLUMN REG_SVC_PKG_VER.DOCUMENT_TYPE_COLUMN IS 'DBF-REG-013 ENT-REG-002.documentTypeColumn — Column of the document type';
COMMENT ON COLUMN REG_SVC_PKG_VER.DOCUMENT_PATH_COLUMN IS 'DBF-REG-014 ENT-REG-002.documentPathColumn — Column of the document location (path)';
COMMENT ON COLUMN REG_SVC_PKG_VER.DOCUMENT_CONTENT_COLUMN IS 'DBF-REG-015 ENT-REG-002.documentContentColumn — Column of the document content (blob)';
COMMENT ON COLUMN REG_SVC_PKG_VER.APPROVAL_ENABLED IS 'DBF-REG-016 ENT-REG-002.approvalEnabled — 1 = approval API enabled';
COMMENT ON COLUMN REG_SVC_PKG_VER.APPROVAL_API IS 'DBF-REG-017 ENT-REG-002.approvalApi — Approval API definition (method + path pattern)';
COMMENT ON COLUMN REG_SVC_PKG_VER.CONTENT_HASH IS 'DBF-REG-018 ENT-REG-002.contentHash — SHA-256 digest of both files (hex)';
COMMENT ON COLUMN REG_SVC_PKG_VER.LOADED_AT IS 'DBF-REG-019 ENT-REG-002.loadedAt — Load run that stored the version';
COMMENT ON COLUMN REG_SVC_PKG_VER.SERVICE_PACKAGE_ID IS 'DBF-REG-020 ENT-REG-002.servicePackageId — FK → REG_SVC_PKG';
COMMENT ON TABLE REG_SVC_QUERY IS 'ENT-REG-003 Service Query — the queries of a version, exactly as written';
COMMENT ON COLUMN REG_SVC_QUERY.SERVICE_QUERY_ID IS 'DBF-REG-021 ENT-REG-003.serviceQueryId — Identifier (identity)';
COMMENT ON COLUMN REG_SVC_QUERY.QUERY_NAME IS 'DBF-REG-022 ENT-REG-003.queryName — Query name — unique per version';
COMMENT ON COLUMN REG_SVC_QUERY.CONNECTION_NAME IS 'DBF-REG-023 ENT-REG-003.connectionName — Connection resolved by name';
COMMENT ON COLUMN REG_SVC_QUERY.SQL_TEXT IS 'DBF-REG-024 ENT-REG-003.sqlText — SQL text — one SELECT, unaltered';
COMMENT ON COLUMN REG_SVC_QUERY.SERVICE_PACKAGE_VERSION_ID IS 'DBF-REG-025 ENT-REG-003.servicePackageVersionId — FK → REG_SVC_PKG_VER';
COMMENT ON TABLE REG_REQ_DOC IS 'ENT-REG-004 Required Document — document types a version requires';
COMMENT ON COLUMN REG_REQ_DOC.REQUIRED_DOCUMENT_ID IS 'DBF-REG-026 ENT-REG-004.requiredDocumentId — Identifier (identity)';
COMMENT ON COLUMN REG_REQ_DOC.DOCUMENT_TYPE IS 'DBF-REG-027 ENT-REG-004.documentType — Document type (DOCUMENT_TYPE)';
COMMENT ON COLUMN REG_REQ_DOC.SERVICE_PACKAGE_VERSION_ID IS 'DBF-REG-028 ENT-REG-004.servicePackageVersionId — FK → REG_SVC_PKG_VER';
COMMENT ON TABLE REG_CONNECTION IS 'ENT-REG-005 Connection — named, read-only data source of this environment';
COMMENT ON COLUMN REG_CONNECTION.CONNECTION_ID IS 'DBF-REG-029 ENT-REG-005.connectionId — Identifier (identity)';
COMMENT ON COLUMN REG_CONNECTION.CONNECTION_NAME IS 'DBF-REG-030 ENT-REG-005.connectionName — Connection name — unique';
COMMENT ON COLUMN REG_CONNECTION.CONNECTION_TYPE IS 'DBF-REG-031 ENT-REG-005.connectionType — mcp | jdbc (CONNECTION_TYPE)';
COMMENT ON COLUMN REG_CONNECTION.ENDPOINT IS 'DBF-REG-032 ENT-REG-005.endpoint — MCP server address or JDBC URL';
COMMENT ON COLUMN REG_CONNECTION.QUERY_TOOL IS 'DBF-REG-033 ENT-REG-005.queryTool — MCP query tool name';
COMMENT ON COLUMN REG_CONNECTION.DIALECT IS 'DBF-REG-034 ENT-REG-005.dialect — SQL dialect';
COMMENT ON COLUMN REG_CONNECTION.CREDENTIAL_REFERENCE IS 'DBF-REG-035 ENT-REG-005.credentialReference — Name of the credential in the secret store — never the secret';
COMMENT ON COLUMN REG_CONNECTION.READ_ONLY IS 'DBF-REG-036 ENT-REG-005.readOnly — Read-only declaration — must be 1';
COMMENT ON COLUMN REG_CONNECTION.LIMITED_TO_VIEWS IS 'DBF-REG-037 ENT-REG-005.limitedToViews — 1 = user limited to specific views';
COMMENT ON COLUMN REG_CONNECTION.ENVIRONMENT_NAME IS 'DBF-REG-038 ENT-REG-005.environmentName — Environment that activated it';
COMMENT ON COLUMN REG_CONNECTION.ACTIVATED_AT IS 'DBF-REG-039 ENT-REG-005.activatedAt — Last activation that registered or updated it';
COMMENT ON TABLE REG_LOAD_RESULT IS 'ENT-REG-006 Load Result — outcomes of the latest load run (load report)';
COMMENT ON COLUMN REG_LOAD_RESULT.LOAD_RESULT_ID IS 'DBF-REG-040 ENT-REG-006.loadResultId — Identifier (identity)';
COMMENT ON COLUMN REG_LOAD_RESULT.LOAD_RUN_AT IS 'DBF-REG-041 ENT-REG-006.loadRunAt — Start of the load run';
COMMENT ON COLUMN REG_LOAD_RESULT.SUBJECT_KIND IS 'DBF-REG-042 ENT-REG-006.subjectKind — SERVICE_PACKAGE | CONNECTION | PACKAGE_DIRECTORY (LOAD_SUBJECT)';
COMMENT ON COLUMN REG_LOAD_RESULT.SUBJECT_NAME IS 'DBF-REG-043 ENT-REG-006.subjectName — Folder name, connection name or package directory path';
COMMENT ON COLUMN REG_LOAD_RESULT.SERVICE_CODE IS 'DBF-REG-044 ENT-REG-006.serviceCode — Service code as declared';
COMMENT ON COLUMN REG_LOAD_RESULT.VERSION_NUMBER IS 'DBF-REG-045 ENT-REG-006.versionNumber — Version number as declared';
COMMENT ON COLUMN REG_LOAD_RESULT.OUTCOME IS 'DBF-REG-046 ENT-REG-006.outcome — Outcome (LOAD_OUTCOME)';
COMMENT ON COLUMN REG_LOAD_RESULT.REASON IS 'DBF-REG-047 ENT-REG-006.reason — Rule message — required when REJECTED';
COMMENT ON COLUMN REG_LOAD_RESULT.CREATED_AT IS 'DBF-REG-048 ENT-REG-006.createdAt — Standard field — profile: entity_defaults.transactional';
COMMENT ON COLUMN REG_LOAD_RESULT.UPDATED_AT IS 'DBF-REG-049 ENT-REG-006.updatedAt — Standard field — profile: entity_defaults.transactional';

-- BLOCK 5a PRIMARY KEYS
ALTER TABLE REG_SVC_PKG ADD CONSTRAINT PK_REG_SVC_PKG PRIMARY KEY (SERVICE_PACKAGE_ID);
ALTER TABLE REG_SVC_PKG_VER ADD CONSTRAINT PK_REG_SVC_PKG_VER PRIMARY KEY (SERVICE_PACKAGE_VERSION_ID);
ALTER TABLE REG_SVC_QUERY ADD CONSTRAINT PK_REG_SVC_QUERY PRIMARY KEY (SERVICE_QUERY_ID);
ALTER TABLE REG_REQ_DOC ADD CONSTRAINT PK_REG_REQ_DOC PRIMARY KEY (REQUIRED_DOCUMENT_ID);
ALTER TABLE REG_CONNECTION ADD CONSTRAINT PK_REG_CONNECTION PRIMARY KEY (CONNECTION_ID);
ALTER TABLE REG_LOAD_RESULT ADD CONSTRAINT PK_REG_LOAD_RESULT PRIMARY KEY (LOAD_RESULT_ID);

-- BLOCK 5b UNIQUE
ALTER TABLE REG_SVC_PKG ADD CONSTRAINT UQ_REG_SVC_PKG_SERVICE_CODE UNIQUE (SERVICE_CODE);                         -- REQ-REG-001, RULE-REG-002 (canonical lower-case codes, so case variants collide — ADR-REG-017)
ALTER TABLE REG_SVC_PKG_VER ADD CONSTRAINT UQ_REG_SVC_PKG_VER_PKG_VERSION UNIQUE (SERVICE_PACKAGE_ID, VERSION_NUMBER); -- RULE-REG-003
ALTER TABLE REG_SVC_QUERY ADD CONSTRAINT UQ_REG_SVC_QUERY_VER_NAME UNIQUE (SERVICE_PACKAGE_VERSION_ID, QUERY_NAME);  -- RULE-REG-019
ALTER TABLE REG_REQ_DOC ADD CONSTRAINT UQ_REG_REQ_DOC_VER_TYPE UNIQUE (SERVICE_PACKAGE_VERSION_ID, DOCUMENT_TYPE);   -- REQ-REG-037; backstop of RULE-REG-021 (REQ-REG-063), which rejects the package first
ALTER TABLE REG_CONNECTION ADD CONSTRAINT UQ_REG_CONNECTION_CONNECTION_NAME UNIQUE (CONNECTION_NAME);              -- RULE-REG-013

-- BLOCK 5c CHECK
ALTER TABLE REG_SVC_PKG ADD CONSTRAINT CHK_REG_SVC_PKG_AVAILABLE CHECK (AVAILABLE IN (0, 1));
ALTER TABLE REG_SVC_PKG ADD CONSTRAINT CHK_REG_SVC_PKG_SERVICE_CODE CHECK (REGEXP_LIKE(SERVICE_CODE, '^[a-z0-9]+(-[a-z0-9]+)*$'));  -- RULE-REG-022, REQ-REG-064 (ADR-REG-017)
ALTER TABLE REG_SVC_PKG_VER ADD CONSTRAINT CHK_REG_SVC_PKG_VER_FETCH_MODE CHECK (FETCH_MODE IN ('path', 'blob', 'manual'));  -- RULE-REG-008
ALTER TABLE REG_SVC_PKG_VER ADD CONSTRAINT CHK_REG_SVC_PKG_VER_VERSION_NUMBER CHECK (VERSION_NUMBER >= 1);
ALTER TABLE REG_SVC_PKG_VER ADD CONSTRAINT CHK_REG_SVC_PKG_VER_APPROVAL_ENABLED CHECK (APPROVAL_ENABLED IN (0, 1));
ALTER TABLE REG_SVC_PKG_VER ADD CONSTRAINT CHK_REG_SVC_PKG_VER_APPROVAL_API CHECK (APPROVAL_ENABLED = 0 OR APPROVAL_API IS NOT NULL);  -- RULE-REG-011
ALTER TABLE REG_SVC_PKG_VER ADD CONSTRAINT CHK_REG_SVC_PKG_VER_DOC_SOURCE CHECK (FETCH_MODE = 'manual' OR (DOCUMENT_SOURCE_QUERY_NAME IS NOT NULL AND DOCUMENT_TYPE_COLUMN IS NOT NULL));  -- RULE-REG-009
ALTER TABLE REG_SVC_PKG_VER ADD CONSTRAINT CHK_REG_SVC_PKG_VER_PATH_COLUMN CHECK (FETCH_MODE <> 'path' OR DOCUMENT_PATH_COLUMN IS NOT NULL);  -- RULE-REG-009
ALTER TABLE REG_SVC_PKG_VER ADD CONSTRAINT CHK_REG_SVC_PKG_VER_CONTENT_COLUMN CHECK (FETCH_MODE <> 'blob' OR DOCUMENT_CONTENT_COLUMN IS NOT NULL);  -- RULE-REG-009
ALTER TABLE REG_CONNECTION ADD CONSTRAINT CHK_REG_CONNECTION_CONNECTION_TYPE CHECK (CONNECTION_TYPE IN ('mcp', 'jdbc'));  -- RULE-REG-014
ALTER TABLE REG_CONNECTION ADD CONSTRAINT CHK_REG_CONNECTION_READ_ONLY CHECK (READ_ONLY = 1);  -- RULE-REG-015
ALTER TABLE REG_CONNECTION ADD CONSTRAINT CHK_REG_CONNECTION_LIMITED_TO_VIEWS CHECK (LIMITED_TO_VIEWS IN (0, 1));
ALTER TABLE REG_CONNECTION ADD CONSTRAINT CHK_REG_CONNECTION_QUERY_TOOL CHECK (CONNECTION_TYPE <> 'mcp' OR QUERY_TOOL IS NOT NULL);
ALTER TABLE REG_LOAD_RESULT ADD CONSTRAINT CHK_REG_LOAD_RESULT_SUBJECT_KIND CHECK (SUBJECT_KIND IN ('SERVICE_PACKAGE', 'CONNECTION', 'PACKAGE_DIRECTORY'));  -- PACKAGE_DIRECTORY: RULE-REG-023 (ADR-REG-018)
ALTER TABLE REG_LOAD_RESULT ADD CONSTRAINT CHK_REG_LOAD_RESULT_OUTCOME CHECK (OUTCOME IN ('REGISTERED', 'UNCHANGED', 'REJECTED', 'WITHDRAWN', 'ACTIVATED', 'UPDATED', 'REMOVED'));
ALTER TABLE REG_LOAD_RESULT ADD CONSTRAINT CHK_REG_LOAD_RESULT_REASON CHECK (OUTCOME <> 'REJECTED' OR REASON IS NOT NULL);

-- BLOCK 5d INTRA-MODULE FOREIGN KEYS
ALTER TABLE REG_SVC_PKG_VER ADD CONSTRAINT FK_REG_SVC_PKG_VER_REG_SVC_PKG FOREIGN KEY (SERVICE_PACKAGE_ID) REFERENCES REG_SVC_PKG (SERVICE_PACKAGE_ID);
ALTER TABLE REG_SVC_QUERY ADD CONSTRAINT FK_REG_SVC_QUERY_REG_SVC_PKG_VER FOREIGN KEY (SERVICE_PACKAGE_VERSION_ID) REFERENCES REG_SVC_PKG_VER (SERVICE_PACKAGE_VERSION_ID);
ALTER TABLE REG_REQ_DOC ADD CONSTRAINT FK_REG_REQ_DOC_REG_SVC_PKG_VER FOREIGN KEY (SERVICE_PACKAGE_VERSION_ID) REFERENCES REG_SVC_PKG_VER (SERVICE_PACKAGE_VERSION_ID);

-- BLOCK 6 TRIGGERS: none — no SRS rule requires one; audit fields are filled by the platform

-- BLOCK 7 INDEXES (non-PK; unique keys are indexed by their constraints)
CREATE INDEX IDX_REG_SVC_PKG_VER_SERVICE_PACKAGE_ID ON REG_SVC_PKG_VER (SERVICE_PACKAGE_ID, LOADED_AT);
CREATE INDEX IDX_REG_SVC_QUERY_SERVICE_PACKAGE_VERSION_ID ON REG_SVC_QUERY (SERVICE_PACKAGE_VERSION_ID, CONNECTION_NAME);
CREATE INDEX IDX_REG_REQ_DOC_SERVICE_PACKAGE_VERSION_ID ON REG_REQ_DOC (SERVICE_PACKAGE_VERSION_ID, REQUIRED_DOCUMENT_ID);
CREATE INDEX IDX_REG_SVC_QUERY_CONNECTION_NAME ON REG_SVC_QUERY (CONNECTION_NAME);
CREATE INDEX IDX_REG_SVC_PKG_AVAILABLE ON REG_SVC_PKG (AVAILABLE);
CREATE INDEX IDX_REG_LOAD_RESULT_LOAD_RUN_AT ON REG_LOAD_RESULT (LOAD_RUN_AT);

-- BLOCK 8 LOOKUP SEED DATA: none
--   Closed keys FETCH_MODE, CONNECTION_TYPE, LOAD_OUTCOME, LOAD_SUBJECT (SERVICE_PACKAGE, CONNECTION, PACKAGE_DIRECTORY) are enforced by the CHECK constraints of BLOCK 5c.
--   Load-run serialization (ADR-REG-015) needs no object: the run takes LOCK TABLE REG_LOAD_RESULT IN EXCLUSIVE MODE inside its one transaction.
--   Open keys SERVICE_CODE and DOCUMENT_TYPE are data: their values (scholarship-request; TRANSCRIPT, ID_CARD)
--   enter REG_SVC_PKG and REG_REQ_DOC when the delivered pilot package folder is loaded at start-up (ADR-REG-010).

-- BLOCK 9 VIEWS: none
-- BLOCK 10 FUNCTIONS / PROCEDURES: none
```

## 5. Decisions applied
| DEFAULT / ADR | What | Source | Override |
|---|---|---|---|
| ADR-REG-010 | Closed lookups → CHECK constraints; open lookups seeded by loading the pilot package, no lookup table | SRS A6; profile conventions.lookups | add a shared lookup table in a later version |
| ADR-REG-003 | Versions immutable: UNIQUE (package, version) + content hash; no delete path | SRS RULE-REG-003 | — |
| ADR-REG-009 | No secret column: credential reference only; READ_ONLY = 1 enforced by CHECK | SRS REQ-REG-054, RULE-REG-015 | — |
| ADR-REG-015 | One load run at a time: the run's single transaction locks REG_LOAD_RESULT in EXCLUSIVE mode — no lock table, no new column | SRS REQ-REG-067, REQ-REG-068 | — |
| ADR-REG-017 | Service codes stored lower case: CHK_REG_SVC_PKG_SERVICE_CODE (REGEXP_LIKE) | SRS REQ-REG-064, RULE-REG-022 | — |
| ADR-REG-018 | LOAD_SUBJECT gains PACKAGE_DIRECTORY: CHK_REG_LOAD_RESULT_SUBJECT_KIND widened | SRS REQ-REG-066, RULE-REG-023 | — |
| DEFAULT — lengths | service code / names 100 CHAR, column names 128 CHAR (oracle19c identifier limit), endpoint / approval API 500 CHAR, reason 1000 CHAR, hash 64 CHAR (SHA-256 hex) | best practice; contract-reg.md key types | widen by migration |
| DEFAULT — load result audit columns | createdAt / updatedAt default SYSTIMESTAMP, filled by the platform | profile entity_defaults.transactional | — |

See registry-db-reg.md.

# FLOW DIAGRAM — Service Registry (REG)
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
Module : REG   Version : v1   Profile : aias   Stage : P3.2 (Part A)
Inputs : srs-reg.md · prd-reg.md · api-spec-reg.yaml · registry-srs-reg.md · registry-exec-be-reg.md
Decisions : ADR-REG-007, ADR-REG-011, ADR-REG-012 (analysis/decisions/REG/)
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

## Flows

None. REG v1 has no screen, so it has no navigation path.

- The SRS declares no screen entry for REG (PART B "Not applicable", SCR-REQ 0): the full administration UI is out of scope (raw idea §2, AIAS-2) [KB:raw-idea.md §2].
- The Service Administrator maintains the package folders and the activation configuration directly, outside the service; a new service package goes live at the next start (ADR-REG-007). No REG story (US-REG-001 … US-REG-013) implies a screen (PRD resolved decision 3).
- The employee frontend of amendment A1 (React + TypeScript) is owned by INT, which holds all five screens and their SCR-REQs [KB:raw-idea.md §15 A1]. Any navigation through a Check, its report, a manual upload or an Employee Decision is an INT flow.
- A flow with no SRS-backed screen would invent navigation (engine A.2); none is drawn (ADR-REG-012).

## What the frontend reaches in REG

REG's only frontend-facing surface is its read API, consumed like any host (A1). It is not a flow: no REG screen is entered, left or navigated.

| Read | API | Traces | Used by |
|---|---|---|---|
| List services | API-REG-001 | REQ-REG-013 | any INT screen that needs the available services (cited there as an INT `UXD-*`) |
| Read one service | API-REG-002 | REQ-REG-014, REQ-REG-015 | INT's Document upload (SCR-REQ-INT-003, REQ-INT-016 — the required document types as the upload's document type choices; cited there as an INT `UXD-*`) |
| Read the load report | API-REG-003 | REQ-REG-008 | no v1 screen — the Service Administrator reads it directly (no administration UI) |

## Reconciliation

```
RECONCILIATION — REG v1
B1 every US-* used in a flow has an SRS counterpart (REQ/AC/screen)   → no flow drawn; no US-REG-* implies a screen
B2 no RULE-* contradicts a flow/spec outcome                           → none (no flow, no screen)
B3 every field/permission on a screen exists in the SRS               → none (no screen)
B4 every screen entry of the SRS has exactly one SCR-* block          → SRS screen entries 0 = SCR blocks 0
RESULT  reconciled 0 · reworked 0 · ADRs ADR-REG-012
```

## 3.0 Binding to the API document

REG's frontend surface is only what a frontend consumes from REG's read API: the three GET operations of `api-spec-reg.yaml`. Method, path, parameters and response schemas (`ServiceSummary`, `LoadResult`, `ProblemDetail`) are read in the document by `x-api-id`, never restated here. The document has no security scheme (raw idea A2).

```yaml name=api-surface
mock: api-spec-reg.yaml
bindings:
  - {req: REQ-REG-013, api: [API-REG-001]}
  - {req: REQ-REG-014, api: [API-REG-002]}
  - {req: REQ-REG-015, api: [API-REG-002]}
  - {req: REQ-REG-008, api: [API-REG-003]}
unmapped: []
codes:
  - {code: "REG-404-SERVICE-NOT-FOUND", rule: RULE-REG-016}
```

Reconciliation against the SRS: every REQ of the SRS "API expectations" table that has an HTTP operation is bound above (REQ-REG-013, REQ-REG-014, REQ-REG-015, REQ-REG-008); every operation of the document maps to a REQ. The in-process operations (supply service package, resolve version, supply connection, supply approval API) have no HTTP operation by design (ADR-REG-011) and are never called by a frontend. `REG-500` is PLATFORM-STD (no RULE) and is routed to the generic server error. No ADR is pending against the document.

<!-- PHASE:F1:START traces=REQ-REG-008,REQ-REG-013,REQ-REG-014,AC-REG-008,AC-REG-014,AC-REG-015,API-REG-001,API-REG-002,API-REG-003 -->
## PHASE F1 — F1 — Models & Types

No screen SUB: REG has no `SCR-*` (SRS PART B not applicable; administration UI out of scope; the employee frontend's screens are INT's — ADR-REG-012). This phase holds REG's client types only.

**RF1 — Models & types.**
Field/DTO binding : see `api-spec-reg.yaml` — the response schemas of API-REG-001, API-REG-002 (`ServiceSummary`) and API-REG-003 (`LoadResult`), and the error body `ProblemDetail`, are the source, not restated here. Types are generated from the document, never hand-written.

| Type | Operation(s) | Rule |
|---|---|---|
| `ServiceSummary` | API-REG-001 (array), API-REG-002 | exactly the document's properties, including the required `available` flag (false for a withdrawn service — ADR-REG-016); it carries no SQL text and no connection setting (REQ-REG-013, AC-REG-014) |
| `LoadResult` | API-REG-003 (array) | exactly the document's properties; nullable `serviceCode`, `versionNumber`, `reason` stay nullable (AC-REG-008) |
| `ProblemDetail` | every error response | `code` carries the catalog code (`REG-404-SERVICE-NOT-FOUND`, `REG-500`) |
| `FetchMode` | `ServiceSummary.fetchMode` | the document's closed enum path · blob · manual (ADR-REG-005) |
| `LoadOutcome`, `LoadSubject` | `LoadResult.outcome`, `LoadResult.subjectKind` | the document's closed enums |

No request type: REG exposes no create, update or delete (REQ-REG-017, ADR-REG-007).
<!-- PHASE:F1:END -->

<!-- PHASE:F2:START traces=REQ-REG-008,REQ-REG-013,REQ-REG-014,REQ-REG-015,AC-REG-008,AC-REG-014,AC-REG-015,AC-REG-016,API-REG-001,API-REG-002,API-REG-003 -->
## PHASE F2 — F2 — Data Hooks

No screen SUB: REG has no `SCR-*` (ADR-REG-012). This phase declares the three read queries a consuming screen (INT's) reuses; INT's own plan binds them in its screens' hook tables. No mutation exists (REQ-REG-017).

The one consumer the SRSs name is INT's Document upload screen requirement (SCR-REQ-INT-003, REQ-INT-016: the document type choices are the required document types of the Check's service, read with GET /api/v1/services/{serviceCode}). The block below names that consuming screen requirement — REG mints no screen — and binds the read it reuses to API-REG-002 (ADR-REG-012):

```yaml name=screen-hooks
screen: SCR-REQ-INT-003
hooks:
  - {hook: SERVICE-QUERY, kind: read, api: [API-REG-002], cache_key: "[reg-service, serviceCode]", errors: "REG-404-SERVICE-NOT-FOUND → user message, text: RULE-REG-016 message (SRS) · REG-500 → generic server error", loading: LOCAL, invalidation: "—"}
```

### SERVICES-QUERY — API-REG-001            traces=API-REG-001,REQ-REG-013
Kind         : read query — method, path and response schema are cited by API-REG-001 (an operation of api-spec-reg.yaml), never restated
Cache key    : ["reg-services"] — the operation takes no filter
Errors       : REG-500 → generic server error
Loading      : LOCAL
Cache policy : defaults
Invalidation : — (read only; the registry changes only at the next start of the service — ADR-REG-007)

### SERVICE-QUERY — API-REG-002             traces=API-REG-002,REQ-REG-014,REQ-REG-015
Kind         : read query — cited by API-REG-002 (an operation of api-spec-reg.yaml), never restated
Cache key    : ["reg-service", serviceCode] — the path parameter is the only input that changes the response
Errors       : REG-404-SERVICE-NOT-FOUND → user message (business rule, no field), text: RULE-REG-016 message (SRS) en "The service "{serviceCode}" is not available." · ar PENDING ADR-REG-011 · REG-500 → generic server error
Loading      : LOCAL
Cache policy : defaults
Invalidation : —
Consumer     : INT's Document upload (SCR-REQ-INT-003, REQ-INT-016)

### LOAD-REPORT-QUERY — API-REG-003         traces=API-REG-003,REQ-REG-008
Kind         : read query — cited by API-REG-003 (an operation of api-spec-reg.yaml), never restated
Cache key    : ["reg-load-results"] — the operation takes no filter
Errors       : REG-500 → generic server error
Loading      : LOCAL
Cache policy : defaults
Invalidation : —

No LOOKUP hook: REG's lookup values reach a frontend only inside these responses. No SCREEN-INIT and no FACADE: there is no REG screen to compose them.
<!-- PHASE:F2:END -->

<!-- PHASE:F3:START traces=REQ-REG-013,REQ-REG-014,AC-REG-014,AC-REG-015,API-REG-001,API-REG-002,API-REG-003 -->
## PHASE F3 — F3 — Forms & Validators

No screen SUB and no form: REG has no `SCR-*` and no write operation, so nothing is entered or submitted (REQ-REG-017, ADR-REG-012). The validators of this phase are the response validators of the three reads:

| Validator | Operation(s) | Behaviour |
|---|---|---|
| `ServiceSummary` schema (strict) | API-REG-001, API-REG-002 | built from the document's schema; a response carrying a property the document does not declare (e.g. `sqlText`, a connection setting) fails validation and is treated as a generic server error — REG responses never expose SQL or connection settings (REQ-REG-013, AC-REG-014) |
| `LoadResult` schema (strict) | API-REG-003 | built from the document's schema; same handling |
| `FetchMode` enum | API-REG-001, API-REG-002 | only path · blob · manual are accepted (AC-REG-015 reads `path`) |
<!-- PHASE:F3:END -->

<!-- PHASE:F4:START traces=REQ-REG-017,AC-REG-018 -->
## PHASE F4 — F4 — Screens & Routes

Empty: REG has no `SCR-*`, so it has no screen, no route, no guard, no facade, no composition and no save (SRS PART B not applicable; administration UI out of scope; the employee frontend's five screens are INT's — ADR-REG-012). No `UXD-*` is cited because none exists.

The frontend holds no affordance and sends no call that creates or changes a REG resource (a service package, a version or a connection): REG exposes read operations only (REQ-REG-017, AC-REG-018, ADR-REG-007).

**RF5 — Security (frontend half).** No permission model — screens open per the SRS (REQ-REG-013, REQ-REG-014, REQ-REG-008 name no role check; caller authentication deferred, raw idea A2).
<!-- PHASE:F4:END -->

<!-- PHASE:ALIGN-FE:START traces=REQ-REG-008,REQ-REG-013,REQ-REG-014,REQ-REG-015,REQ-REG-017,API-REG-001,API-REG-002,API-REG-003 -->

| Lookup key | Owner code |

| Entity | Owner code | HARD-FK / SOFT-READ | Why |
|---|---|---|---|
| None — REG is the root of the platform graph | — | — | platform-summary DEPENDENCIES |

DEPENDENCIES
| Module code | HARD-FK / SOFT-READ | What is consumed |
|---|---|---|
| None | — | — |
ROOT: YES

CONSUMERS (context — the edges belong to the consuming modules)
| Module code | What it reads from REG | Source |
|---|---|---|
| CHK | service package (service knowledge, queries, required documents, version, approval flag), connections | domain-profile §6 |

POLICIES (business-policies-reg.md)
| Policy | Short name |
|---|---|
| POL-REG-001 | One service package per service |
| POL-REG-002 | Service knowledge and service definition kept apart |
| POL-REG-003 | A new service needs no code |
| POL-REG-004 | Only the service administrator supplies service packages |
| POL-REG-005 | Every service package carries a version |
| POL-REG-006 | Service knowledge is read whole |
| POL-REG-007 | Queries are exactly those in the service definition |
| POL-REG-008 | Fetch mode and required documents per service |
| POL-REG-009 | The approval API is optional per service |
| POL-REG-010 | Connections defined once, outside the service packages |
| POL-REG-011 | Connections set per environment at activation |
| POL-REG-012 | Host data only through read-only connections |
| POL-REG-013 | Service codes come only from the service registry |
| POL-REG-014 | Scholarship request is the pilot service package |
| POL-REG-015 | The service registry holds no request data |

AUTO-DECISIONS
AUTO: Service Knowledge and Service Definition are parts of the Service Package entity, not separate entities  FROM: project-registry §4 notes; domain-profile §7.1  IF WRONG: P1 registers them as two entities owned by REG
AUTO: The version is carried by the Service Package; whether a version is an entity of its own is left to P1  FROM: [KB:raw-idea.md §4]; G11  IF WRONG: P1 registers a Service Package Version entity owned by REG
AUTO: Service Package and Connection are SHARED (read through the REG interface by CHK, DOC, RPT, INT)  FROM: project-registry §5; domain-profile §6  IF WRONG: mark PRIVATE and route all reads through CHK
AUTO: A loaded service package version is never changed in place; a change is a new version; a new Check uses the current version; every loaded version stays resolvable so a stored report's version can be traced to its content  FROM: [KB:raw-idea.md §4]; G11; ADR-REG-003  IF WRONG: allow in-place edits and record only the version number
AUTO: The read-only JDBC data source used by `blob` fetching is a Connection of type `jdbc` held in REG  FROM: [KB:raw-idea.md §6]; G14; ADR-REG-004  IF WRONG: DOC holds its own JDBC data source outside REG
AUTO: Fetch mode values are the profile's closed enum; REG accepts only `path`, `blob`, `manual` in a service definition without reading DOC  FROM: profile `conventions.lookups`; ADR-REG-005  IF WRONG: REG soft-reads the list from DOC (would break REG's tier 0)
AUTO: The allowed storage root (G5) is DOC's environment setting and the check limits (G8) are platform configuration (`aias.check.timeout` PT2M, `aias.check.max-rows` 100, `aias.check.max-file-size` 10MB — ADR-REG-019); neither is REG data  FROM: [KB:raw-idea.md §12]; profile tracks.backend CORE; ADR-REG-006; ADR-REG-019  IF WRONG: add them to the Connection or the service definition
AUTO: No administration UI; the service administrator maintains service packages and connections directly  FROM: [KB:raw-idea.md §2]; profile review AIAS-2  IF WRONG: a later version adds the administration UI

<<<ARTIFACT: current-platform-summary.md>>>
# PLATFORM SUMMARY — Request Verification Service
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
Profile : aias   Domain profile : v1   Registry : v1.0.0
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

## OVERVIEW
The Request Verification Service is a standalone Spring Boot service (Java 21, Spring Boot 4, Spring AI 2.0) that a host system calls over REST to verify one government service request before an employee approves it [KB:raw-idea.md §1, §3]. For each Check it loads the service package of the requested service, runs the queries the service definition names through a read-only MCP connection, fetches the request's documents by the service's fetch mode (`path`, `blob` or `manual`), reads them by type, runs the deterministic checks in code, lets the LLM compare the data and document content with the service knowledge, and stores a structured, evidence-backed report in the service's own schema [KB:raw-idea.md §5, §7, §9]. The employee reviews the report in a React frontend embedded in the host screen and records the Employee Decision; where a service enables it, the service then calls the host's Approval API — only after the employee confirms [KB:raw-idea.md §11, §15 A1]. The service never makes the decision. Every service it verifies is configuration, not code, so the platform's foundation is the Service Registry that holds those packages and the connections they use (domain-profile §2, §4). Caller authentication is deferred to a later version; the §12 guardrails are part of this version (domain-profile D7).

Ownership of the run records (closes registry OQ-1 and OQ-2 — owner statement, raw idea §14 "Runs, findings, documents" under RPT):
- RPT owns the Check run record, the findings and the Check Document record.
- CHK runs the pipeline and writes its results through RPT's interface.
- DOC fetches and reads documents but does not own their stored rows.

## DEPENDENCIES — data, not prose (`gov.py graph` reads this named block; the build order is DERIVED from it; C0.1 holds it to its schema)
```yaml name=platform-dependencies
modules:
  REG: {tier: 0, depends_on: []}
  DOC: {tier: 1, depends_on: [REG]}
  CHK: {tier: 2, depends_on: [REG, DOC]}
  RPT: {tier: 3, depends_on: [CHK]}
  INT: {tier: 4, depends_on: [CHK, RPT, DOC]}
```
One entry per module of the platform, every one with its tier; `depends_on` lists the modules it reads from (a lower tier never depends on a higher one — `xm.tier_rule`). The build order, the waves and the critical path are computed from this block (`gov.py plan-order`); never written by hand.

Reconciliation with domain-profile §6 (owner graph kept; no edge changed):
- §6 row "DOC depends on INT (manual upload received through the API), direction INT → DOC" states the data flow of a manual upload. In code INT receives the upload and hands it to DOC through DOC's interface, so the compile-time edge is INT → DOC (INT depends_on DOC), which is the owner's graph. Reading the row as DOC depends_on INT would create the cycle DOC → INT → DOC. Decision #3.
- §6 row "RPT depends on CHK" agrees with the owner's graph. The owner's OQ-1 answer makes CHK write the run results through RPT's interface; that runtime call is carried by a result port that CHK declares and RPT implements, so the compile-time edge stays RPT → CHK and the graph stays acyclic. Decision #4 (ADR-REG-002).
- DOC depends_on REG is the owner's statement; §6 does not list it and does not contradict it (DOC reads the fetch mode and required documents from the service definition and the `blob` connection from REG).

## DEFERRED (not in scope for this version)

| Workflow engine | profile: `forbidden` — the check pipeline is fixed code [KB:raw-idea.md §5] |
| Caller authentication (API key or mTLS) and the security phases | owner amendment A2 (D7) — returns with the security version; the owner already has the solution |
| Who may view stored reports | deferred with caller authentication (D4, A2) |
| Full administration UI and an internal permission system | out of scope [KB:raw-idea.md §2] — the service administrator maintains service packages directly |
| Multi-tenancy | single-tenancy deployment [KB:raw-idea.md §2, §13] |
| Conversation memory | a Check is one independent run (G9) [KB:raw-idea.md §2] |
| RAG and a vector store | service knowledge fits whole in the prompt; activation trigger: one service's knowledge grows to hundreds of pages [KB:raw-idea.md §2] |
| Multi-agent orchestration | out of scope [KB:raw-idea.md §2] |
| Server-rendered report page (`GET /checks/{id}/view`) | superseded by the employee frontend (A1, D6) |
| LLM provider for real request data | go-live gate (D3); no design element waits on it — free tier with synthetic / anonymised data only until then (G13) |

## USER STORIES

US-REG-001
  Title          : One service package per service
  Story          : As a service administrator, I need to describe each service as one service package made of its service knowledge and its service definition, kept as separate parts, so that the conditions are interpreted by the LLM while queries and document locations are executed exactly as I wrote them.
  Priority       : —
  Success metric : —
  Traces         : POL-REG-001, POL-REG-002
  Source         : [KB:raw-idea.md §4] "one package per service … The two files are separate on purpose"; business-policies-reg POL-REG-001, POL-REG-002
  Status         : DRAFT

US-REG-002
  Title          : Add a service without code
  Story          : As a service administrator, I need to add a new service by adding its service package, with its service code coming from the service registry alone, so that a new service that uses existing check types goes live without a code change or a release.
  Priority       : —
  Success metric : —
  Traces         : POL-REG-003, POL-REG-013
  Source         : [KB:raw-idea.md §1, §4] "Adding a service needs no code as long as it uses existing check types"; profile `conventions.lookups`
  Status         : DRAFT

US-REG-003
  Title          : Service knowledge comes only from the service administrator
  Story          : As an employee, I need the conditions a Check applies to come only from the service package the service administrator maintains, so that I never have to supply or vouch for service knowledge myself.
  Priority       : —
  Success metric : —
  Traces         : POL-REG-004
  Source         : [KB:raw-idea.md §2] "Employees never supply knowledge files"; business-policies-reg POL-REG-004
  Status         : DRAFT

US-REG-004
  Title          : Every service package change is a new version
  Story          : As a service administrator, I need every service package to carry a version that each Check receives, so that every report can be traced to the exact service knowledge and service definition it was built on.
  Priority       : —
  Success metric : —
  Traces         : POL-REG-005
  Source         : [KB:raw-idea.md §4] "Each package carries a version, and every report records the version it was built on"; domain-profile G11; ADR-REG-003
  Status         : DRAFT

US-REG-005
  Title          : Service knowledge read whole
  Story          : As a service administrator, I need each Check to receive the whole service knowledge of its service, so that no condition is missed because only part of the knowledge was retrieved.
  Priority       : —
  Success metric : —
  Traces         : POL-REG-006
  Source         : [KB:raw-idea.md §2] "each service's knowledge fits whole in the prompt, which is more reliable for compliance than retrieving fragments"
  Status         : DRAFT

US-REG-006
  Title          : Queries exactly as written in the service definition
  Story          : As a service administrator, I need a Check to use only the queries I wrote in the service definition, each against its named connection and with its parameters supplied safely rather than pasted into the SQL, so that no query is ever invented, altered or built from free text.
  Priority       : HIGH
  Success metric : —
  Traces         : POL-REG-007
  Source         : [KB:raw-idea.md §4, §6, §12]; [KB:raw-idea.md §0] "Section 12 Guardrails are non-negotiable"; domain-profile G1, G4
  Status         : DRAFT

US-REG-007
  Title          : Fetch mode and required documents per service
  Story          : As a service administrator, I need to state for each service how its documents are obtained — `path`, `blob` or `manual` — and which documents it requires, so that every Check fetches the right documents and knows which ones must be present.
  Priority       : —
  Success metric : —
  Traces         : POL-REG-008
  Source         : [KB:raw-idea.md §4, §6] "All three modes are available, chosen per service"; §13 Decided "Documents"
  Status         : DRAFT

US-REG-008
  Title          : Approval API enabled per service
  Story          : As a service administrator, I need to state for each service whether the host's approval API may be called after the employee confirms a decision, so that services without that API keep approval in the host system as today.
  Priority       : —
  Success metric : —
  Traces         : POL-REG-009
  Source         : [KB:raw-idea.md §4 `approval.enabled`, §11] "Two options, chosen per service"; domain-profile G2
  Status         : DRAFT

US-REG-009
  Title          : Connections defined once and named
  Story          : As a service administrator, I need to define each connection once, outside the service packages, and refer to it by name from any service definition, so that a data source is configured in one place and shared by every service that uses it.
  Priority       : —
  Success metric : —
  Traces         : POL-REG-010
  Source         : [KB:raw-idea.md §4] "Connections are defined once, outside the service packages"
  Status         : DRAFT

US-REG-010
  Title          : Connections set per environment at activation
  Story          : As a service administrator, I need to set the connections of each environment when the service is activated there, so that the same service packages run unchanged in every environment.
  Priority       : —
  Success metric : —
  Traces         : POL-REG-011
  Source         : [KB:raw-idea.md §4] "set at activation time for each environment"; §13 Decided "Database access … specified at activation"
  Status         : DRAFT

US-REG-011
  Title          : Host data reached read-only
  Story          : As a service administrator, I need every connection to host data — through MCP or, for `blob` documents, through JDBC — to use a read-only database user, preferably limited to specific views, so that the service can never change a host system's data.
  Priority       : HIGH
  Success metric : —
  Traces         : POL-REG-012
  Source         : [KB:raw-idea.md §6, §12]; [KB:raw-idea.md §0] guardrails non-negotiable; domain-profile G3, G14; ADR-REG-004
  Status         : DRAFT

US-REG-012
  Title          : Scholarship request as the pilot service
  Story          : As an employee who approves scholarship requests, I need the scholarship request — with its explicit numeric conditions, its required TRANSCRIPT and ID_CARD documents and the `path` fetch mode — to be the first service package available, so that the first verified requests are the ones I handle.
  Priority       : HIGH
  Success metric : —
  Traces         : POL-REG-014
  Source         : domain-profile §8 D5 "Scholarship request — the raw idea's own example" (owner-confirmed 2026-10-01); [KB:raw-idea.md §4]
  Status         : DRAFT

US-REG-013
  Title          : No request data kept in the service registry
  Story          : As an employee, I need the service registry to hold only service configuration and no request data, so that nothing from one request can reach the Check of another.
  Priority       : HIGH
  Success metric : —
  Traces         : POL-REG-015
  Source         : [KB:raw-idea.md §2, §12] "No data is carried from one check to another"; domain-profile G9
  Status         : DRAFT

# PART A — MODULE FOUNDATION

## A1 — Document information
| Item | Value |
|---|---|
| Module | REG — Service Registry |
| Feature code | REG |
| Version | v1 |
| Date | 2026-10-01 |
| Status | DRAFT — P1 output, PRD approved 2026-10-01 (gate prd-approval) |
| Prepared by | P1 SRS engine (operator run, lane analysis) |
| Decisions applied | 16 ADRs (ADR-REG-001 … ADR-REG-009, ADR-REG-013 … ADR-REG-019) and 4 DEFAULTs — see Decisions applied |
| Revision | gate-analysis REVISE 2026-10-01: REQ-REG-063 … REQ-REG-069, AC-REG-065 … AC-REG-081, RULE-REG-021 … RULE-REG-024 added; REQ-REG-013, REQ-REG-014, AC-REG-014, AC-REG-015 extended with `available` (ADR-REG-016) |

## A2 — Functional context

### In scope
- Service packages: one per service, each made of the service knowledge and the service definition, versioned and never edited in place (POL-REG-001 … POL-REG-008, ADR-REG-003).
- Loading service packages from the Service Administrator's package directory at start-up, with a load report of every outcome (ADR-REG-007).
- Connections of type `mcp` and `jdbc`, defined once and set per environment at activation, read-only (POL-REG-010 … POL-REG-012, ADR-REG-004, ADR-REG-009).
- Read operations: the list of services, one service's current version summary, the load report; in-process supply of service packages and connections to CHK, DOC, RPT and INT.
- The scholarship request pilot package (POL-REG-014, D5).

### Out of scope
- A full administration UI and an internal permission system (scope exceptions; AIAS-2). Caller authentication is deferred (raw-idea A2) — no requirement here depends on it.
- Multi-tenancy, conversation memory, RAG and vector stores, multi-agent orchestration.
- Running queries, fetching or reading documents, comparing with the LLM, storing reports, calling the Approval API — CHK, DOC, RPT and INT (ADR-REG-001).
- The allowed storage root and the per-Check limits (timeout, maximum rows, maximum file size) — DOC environment setting and platform configuration (ADR-REG-006): `aias.check.timeout` (default PT2M), `aias.check.max-rows` (default 100), `aias.check.max-file-size` (default 10MB), owned by the CORE phase of CHK and DOC; REG reads none of them (ADR-REG-019).

### Module function
The Service Registry holds what each service IS: its Service Package — the service knowledge the LLM reads whole and the service definition the engine executes literally (queries with their named connection, fetch mode, required documents, optional Approval API) — in immutable versions, and the connections each environment activates. Every Check obtains its service package and connections from it; nothing else in the platform is a source of service configuration or service codes.

### Detailed description
The Service Administrator keeps one folder per service in the package directory (raw idea §4 layout). At every start the service activates the environment's connections from its activation configuration, then loads every folder: it validates the package, stores a new version when the content is new, keeps the stored version when it is identical, rejects an invalid or in-place-edited package, and marks withdrawn a service whose folder disappeared. Every outcome becomes a Load Result row. At Check time CHK asks for the current version of a service code and receives the service knowledge, the queries and the document settings as separate, read-only parts; DOC reads the fetch mode, required document types and the `jdbc` connection; INT's Employee Decision path alone reads the Approval API definition; RPT records the version number. Roles: Service Administrator (maintains folders and activation configuration), Employee and Host System (read services only).

### Current situation
| Step | Party | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| Employee checks request data and documents against the service's conditions by hand | Employee | No configuration exists; conditions live in people's heads and circulars [KB:raw-idea.md §1] |

### Current difficulties
Each service would otherwise need its own code; conditions, queries and document rules are not versioned, so a decision cannot be traced to the rules it used [KB:raw-idea.md §1, §4].

### Proposed system and benefits
Services become configuration: a new service using existing check types goes live by adding a folder and restarting, with no code change or release (US-REG-002); every report is traceable to an immutable version (US-REG-004); host data is reached only through read-only, named connections (US-REG-011).

### General notes
- Logical types only; physical types and tables belong to P2.
- §12 guardrails are stated here at the surface REG controls (ADR-REG-008).
- A new package goes live at the next start of the service (ADR-REG-007).
- One load run at a time: the whole run is one transaction under one exclusive database lock (ADR-REG-015).
- An unreachable or empty package directory withdraws nothing (ADR-REG-018).

## A3 — Entities and fields

Standard fields — per profile: kind `transactional` carries `createdAt, updatedAt` (system-filled, never accepted from a client); kind `config` carries none. Every identifier field is a number key generated by identity in the service's own schema (profile `conventions.identifiers`); host identifiers are never stored here (REQ-REG-059).

### ENT-REG-001 — Service Package
Kind reason: config — registry data maintained by the Service Administrator, read by every Check.

| Kind | Ownership | Business number | Operations | Cross-module | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| config | SHARED (owner) — read by CHK, DOC, RPT, INT through the REG interface | yes — serviceCode | create (load), read, update (withdraw / make available); no delete | SOFT-READ by CHK, DOC, RPT, INT | POL-REG-001, POL-REG-013; module-registry |

| Field | Logical type | Required | Values / source | Notes | Label |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| servicePackageId | number (identifier) | yes | identity | system key | Service package id |
| serviceCode | text | yes | lookup SERVICE_CODE (this entity is its source) | unique; as declared by the package folder, trimmed and stored in canonical lower case, compared case-insensitively (REQ-REG-064, RULE-REG-022, ADR-REG-017); immutable | Service code |
| available | flag | yes | true / false | false while withdrawn (REQ-REG-010) | Available |
| registeredAt | date-time | yes | system | first load of the service code | Registered at |
| withdrawnAt | date-time | no | system | set when withdrawn, cleared when available again | Withdrawn at |

### ENT-REG-002 — Service Package Version
Kind reason: config — registry data maintained by the Service Administrator, read by every Check.

| Kind | Ownership | Business number | Operations | Cross-module | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| config | SHARED (owner) | yes — serviceCode + versionNumber | create (load), read; never updated or deleted (ADR-REG-003) | SOFT-READ by CHK, DOC, RPT, INT | POL-REG-002, POL-REG-005, POL-REG-008, POL-REG-009; ADR-REG-003 |

| Field | Logical type | Required | Values / source | Notes | Label |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| servicePackageVersionId | number (identifier) | yes | identity | system key | Version id |
| servicePackageId | reference | yes | ENT-REG-001 | owning Service Package | Service package |
| versionNumber | number | yes | declared `version` | unique per Service Package | Version |
| serviceKnowledge | text | yes | knowledge file, whole | non-empty (RULE-REG-018); supplied unaltered | Service knowledge |
| serviceDefinition | text | yes | definition file, whole | kept as loaded for resolvability | Service definition |
| inputName | text | yes | declared `input`, e.g. `requestId` | the only bind parameter name (RULE-REG-006) | Input |
| fetchMode | lookup | yes | FETCH_MODE | path | blob | manual (ADR-REG-005) | Fetch mode |
| documentSourceQueryName | text | no | a queryName of ENT-REG-003 of the same version | required for path / blob | Document source query |
| documentTypeColumn | text | no | column of the document source query | required for path / blob | Document type column |
| documentPathColumn | text | no | column of the document source query | required for path | Path column |
| documentContentColumn | text | no | column of the document source query | required for blob | Content column |
| approvalEnabled | flag | yes | declared `approval.enabled` | default false | Approval API enabled |
| approvalApi | text | no | declared `approval.api` (method + path pattern) | required when approvalEnabled (RULE-REG-011) | Approval API |
| contentHash | text | yes | system — digest of both files | detects in-place edits (RULE-REG-003) | Content hash |
| loadedAt | date-time | yes | system | time of the load run that stored it | Loaded at |

### ENT-REG-003 — Service Query
Kind reason: config — registry data maintained by the Service Administrator, read by every Check.

| Kind | Ownership | Business number | Operations | Cross-module | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| config | SHARED (owner) — read by CHK | no | create (with its version), read | SOFT-READ by CHK, DOC | POL-REG-007; [KB:raw-idea.md §4] |

| Field | Logical type | Required | Values / source | Notes | Label |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| serviceQueryId | number (identifier) | yes | identity | system key | Query id |
| servicePackageVersionId | reference | yes | ENT-REG-002 | owning version | Version |
| queryName | text | yes | declared query key | unique per version (RULE-REG-019) | Query name |
| connectionName | text | yes | connectionName of ENT-REG-005 | resolved by name (RULE-REG-005) | Connection |
| sqlText | text | yes | declared `sql` | one SELECT statement; named bind parameter of inputName only; supplied unaltered | SQL |

### ENT-REG-004 — Required Document
Kind reason: config — registry data maintained by the Service Administrator, read by every Check.

| Kind | Ownership | Business number | Operations | Cross-module | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| config | SHARED (owner) — read by CHK, DOC | no | create (with its version), read | SOFT-READ by CHK, DOC | POL-REG-008, POL-REG-014 |

| Field | Logical type | Required | Values / source | Notes | Label |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| requiredDocumentId | number (identifier) | yes | identity | system key | Required document id |
| servicePackageVersionId | reference | yes | ENT-REG-002 | owning version | Version |
| documentType | lookup | yes | DOCUMENT_TYPE | value as the host's document type column returns it; unique per version | Document type |

### ENT-REG-005 — Connection
Kind reason: config — registry data maintained by the Service Administrator, read by every Check.

| Kind | Ownership | Business number | Operations | Cross-module | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| config | SHARED (owner) — read by CHK, DOC | yes — connectionName | create (activation), read, update (activation), delete (activation) | SOFT-READ by CHK, DOC | POL-REG-010 … POL-REG-012; ADR-REG-004; ADR-REG-009 |

| Field | Logical type | Required | Values / source | Notes | Label |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| connectionId | number (identifier) | yes | identity | system key | Connection id |
| connectionName | text | yes | activation configuration | unique (RULE-REG-013) | Connection name |
| connectionType | lookup | yes | CONNECTION_TYPE | mcp | jdbc (RULE-REG-014) | Type |
| endpoint | text | yes | activation configuration | MCP server address or JDBC URL | Endpoint |
| queryTool | text | no | activation configuration | required for mcp, e.g. `run_query` | Query tool |
| dialect | text | yes | activation configuration | e.g. `oracle` | Dialect |
| credentialReference | text | yes | activation configuration | name in the environment's secret store; never the secret (REQ-REG-054) | Credential reference |
| readOnly | flag | yes | activation configuration | must be true (RULE-REG-015) | Read-only user |
| limitedToViews | flag | yes | activation configuration | default false | Limited to views |
| environmentName | text | yes | activation configuration | environment that activated it | Environment |
| activatedAt | date-time | yes | system | last activation that registered or updated it | Activated at |

### ENT-REG-006 — Load Result
Kind reason: transactional — one row per outcome of a load run, replaced at every run.

| Kind | Ownership | Business number | Operations | Cross-module | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| transactional | PRIVATE | no | create (load run), read, delete (next load run) | — | ADR-REG-007; [KB:raw-idea.md §12] |

| Field | Logical type | Required | Values / source | Notes | Label |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| loadResultId | number (identifier) | yes | identity | system key | Load result id |
| loadRunAt | date-time | yes | system | start time of the load run | Load run |
| subjectKind | lookup | yes | LOAD_SUBJECT | SERVICE_PACKAGE | CONNECTION | PACKAGE_DIRECTORY (ADR-REG-018) | Subject kind |
| subjectName | text | yes | folder name, connection name or package directory path |  | Subject |
| serviceCode | text | no | as declared by the folder | absent when unreadable | Service code |
| versionNumber | number | no | as declared by the folder |  | Version |
| outcome | lookup | yes | LOAD_OUTCOME |  | Outcome |
| reason | text | no | the RULE message | required when outcome = REJECTED | Reason |
| createdAt, updatedAt | date-time | yes | system | standard fields — per profile | Created at, Updated at |

## A4 — Functional requirements (EARS) and acceptance criteria

### REQ-REG-001 — One service package per service code
  Pattern    : ubiquitous
  Statement  : The system shall hold exactly one Service Package per service code.
  Traces     : US-REG-001
  Entities   : ENT-REG-001
  Rationale  : Everything that differs between services lives in one package per service.
  Source     : POL-REG-001; [KB:raw-idea.md §4]
  Priority   : —

#### AC-REG-001 — [REQ-REG-001]
  Given  : the package directory holds the folder `scholarship-request` and the registry holds no service
  When   : the service starts
  Then   : the registry stores 1 Service Package with serviceCode `scholarship-request`

### REQ-REG-002 — Two separate parts per version
  Pattern    : ubiquitous
  Statement  : The system shall store every service package version as two separate parts: the service knowledge text and the service definition.
  Traces     : US-REG-001
  Entities   : ENT-REG-002
  Rationale  : SQL and file locations are executed literally; only the conditions are interpreted by the LLM.
  Source     : POL-REG-002; [KB:raw-idea.md §4]
  Priority   : —

#### AC-REG-002 — [REQ-REG-002]
  Given  : a valid package folder with a service knowledge file and a service definition file
  When   : the package is loaded
  Then   : the stored version holds serviceKnowledge equal to the knowledge file text and serviceDefinition equal to the definition file text, as 2 separate fields

### REQ-REG-003 — Incomplete package rejected
  Pattern    : unwanted
  Statement  : If a service package folder lacks its service knowledge file or its service definition file, then the system shall reject that package.
  Traces     : US-REG-001
  Entities   : ENT-REG-002, ENT-REG-006
  Rationale  : A package missing either part cannot be verified against.
  Source     : POL-REG-001, POL-REG-002; RULE-REG-001
  Priority   : —
  Rules      : applies RULE-REG-001

#### AC-REG-003 — [REQ-REG-003]
  Given  : a package folder `demo-service` that holds only a service definition file
  When   : the service starts
  Then   : the package is rejected, no version is stored for it, and the load report records outcome REJECTED with the RULE-REG-001 message

### REQ-REG-004 — Duplicate service code rejected
  Pattern    : unwanted
  Statement  : If two service package folders declare the same service code, then the system shall reject every package that declares that service code.
  Traces     : US-REG-001, US-REG-002
  Entities   : ENT-REG-001, ENT-REG-006
  Rationale  : One package per service; the registry cannot choose between two.
  Source     : POL-REG-001, POL-REG-013; RULE-REG-002
  Priority   : —
  Rules      : applies RULE-REG-002

#### AC-REG-004 — [REQ-REG-004]
  Given  : folders `a` and `b` both declare service code `scholarship-request` and version 4, and version 3 is stored
  When   : the service starts
  Then   : both packages are rejected with the RULE-REG-002 message and version 3 stays the current version

### REQ-REG-005 — Load the package directory at start
  Pattern    : event
  Statement  : When the service starts, the system shall load every service package folder of the configured package directory.
  Traces     : US-REG-002
  Entities   : ENT-REG-001, ENT-REG-002, ENT-REG-003, ENT-REG-004
  Rationale  : A new service goes live by adding its folder, with no code change and no release. The Service Administrator publishes a folder by writing it elsewhere and renaming it into the package directory (atomic rename), so the load run never reads a half-written file; a file that changes while it is read is rejected (REQ-REG-069).
  Source     : POL-REG-003; ADR-REG-007
  Priority   : —

#### AC-REG-005 — [REQ-REG-005]
  Given  : the package directory holds 3 package folders
  When   : the service starts
  Then   : the load report contains 3 Load Result rows of subject kind SERVICE_PACKAGE, one per folder

### REQ-REG-006 — Register a new service code
  Pattern    : event
  Statement  : When a loaded service package declares a service code that the registry does not hold, the system shall register the service code with that package as its first version.
  Traces     : US-REG-002
  Entities   : ENT-REG-001, ENT-REG-002
  Rationale  : The service registry is the only source of service codes.
  Source     : POL-REG-003, POL-REG-013
  Priority   : —

#### AC-REG-006 — [REQ-REG-006]
  Given  : the registry holds no service code `vehicle-permit` and a valid folder declares `vehicle-permit` version 1
  When   : the service starts
  Then   : the registry creates Service Package `vehicle-permit` with available = true and stores version 1 as its current version, and the load report records outcome REGISTERED

### REQ-REG-007 — Rejected items recorded in the load report
  Pattern    : unwanted
  Statement  : If a service package or a connection fails validation, then the system shall reject it, record the reason in the load report and continue loading the remaining items.
  Traces     : US-REG-002, US-REG-003
  Entities   : ENT-REG-006
  Rationale  : Raw idea §12: anything that could not be read appears; nothing is skipped silently.
  Source     : [KB:raw-idea.md §12]; ADR-REG-007; ADR-REG-008
  Priority   : HIGH

#### AC-REG-007 — [REQ-REG-007]
  Given  : the package directory holds 2 valid folders and 1 folder whose service definition names fetch mode `fax`
  When   : the service starts
  Then   : 2 packages are stored, the third is rejected, and the load report records 3 rows: 2 REGISTERED and 1 REJECTED with the RULE-REG-008 message

#### AC-REG-078 — [REQ-REG-007]
  Given  : the activation configuration lists `main-db` (mcp, read-only) and `bad-db` declared readOnly = false, and the package directory holds 1 valid folder naming `main-db` and 1 folder declaring `fetch: fax`
  When   : the service starts
  Then   : in the same load run `main-db` is registered (ACTIVATED), `bad-db` is not (REJECTED with the RULE-REG-015 message), the valid package is stored (REGISTERED) and the other is rejected (REJECTED with the RULE-REG-008 message) — 4 rows

### REQ-REG-008 — Read the load report
  Pattern    : event
  Statement  : When the Service Administrator requests the load report, the system shall return the subject kind, subject name, version number, outcome and reason of every Load Result of the most recent load run.
  Traces     : US-REG-002
  Entities   : ENT-REG-006
  Rationale  : The administrator sees why a package did not go live.
  Source     : ADR-REG-007
  Priority   : —

#### AC-REG-008 — [REQ-REG-008]
  Given  : the most recent load run recorded 1 REGISTERED and 1 REJECTED result
  When   : the Service Administrator requests the load report
  Then   : the system returns 2 rows, each with subjectKind, subjectName, versionNumber, outcome and reason

### REQ-REG-009 — Previous load results removed at a new run
  Pattern    : event
  Statement  : When a new load run starts, the system shall delete the Load Result rows of the previous load run.
  Traces     : US-REG-002
  Entities   : ENT-REG-006
  Rationale  : The load report describes the packages and connections as they stand now.
  Source     : ADR-REG-007
  Priority   : —

#### AC-REG-009 — [REQ-REG-009]
  Given  : the previous load run left 4 Load Result rows
  When   : the service starts and loads 2 folders and 1 connection
  Then   : the load report contains exactly 3 rows, all with the loadRunAt of the new run

### REQ-REG-010 — Withdraw a service whose folder is gone
  Pattern    : event
  Statement  : When a service code held by the registry has no folder in the package directory at load, the system shall mark that Service Package withdrawn.
  Traces     : US-REG-002
  Entities   : ENT-REG-001, ENT-REG-006
  Rationale  : Retiring a service stays a configuration act; stored versions stay resolvable.
  Source     : POL-REG-013; ADR-REG-003; ADR-REG-007
  Priority   : —

#### AC-REG-010 — [REQ-REG-010]
  Given  : Service Package `vehicle-permit` is available and its folder was removed
  When   : the service starts
  Then   : the registry sets available = false and withdrawnAt on `vehicle-permit`, keeps its stored versions, and records outcome WITHDRAWN

### REQ-REG-011 — Make a returning service available again
  Pattern    : event
  Statement  : When a withdrawn service code has a valid folder in the package directory at load, the system shall mark that Service Package available.
  Traces     : US-REG-002
  Entities   : ENT-REG-001
  Rationale  : Restoring a folder restores the service without any code change.
  Source     : POL-REG-003; ADR-REG-007
  Priority   : —

#### AC-REG-011 — [REQ-REG-011]
  Given  : Service Package `vehicle-permit` is withdrawn and its valid folder is back
  When   : the service starts
  Then   : the registry sets available = true and clears withdrawnAt on `vehicle-permit`

### REQ-REG-012 — No package for a withdrawn or unknown service
  Pattern    : unwanted
  Statement  : If a Check requests the service package of a withdrawn service code or of a service code the registry does not hold, then the system shall refuse to supply a service package.
  Traces     : US-REG-002
  Entities   : ENT-REG-001
  Rationale  : A Check runs only against a service the registry offers.
  Source     : POL-REG-013; RULE-REG-016
  Priority   : —
  Rules      : applies RULE-REG-016

#### AC-REG-012 — [REQ-REG-012]
  Given  : Service Package `vehicle-permit` is withdrawn
  When   : a Check requests the service package of `vehicle-permit`
  Then   : the system supplies no package and returns the RULE-REG-016 message

#### AC-REG-013 — [REQ-REG-012]
  Given  : the registry holds no service code `unknown-service`
  When   : a Check requests the service package of `unknown-service`
  Then   : the system supplies no package and returns the RULE-REG-016 message

### REQ-REG-013 — List the services
  Pattern    : event
  Statement  : When a Host System or the Service Administrator requests the list of services, the system shall return the service code, availability flag, current version number, fetch mode, required document types and approval API flag of every available Service Package.
  Traces     : US-REG-002
  Entities   : ENT-REG-001, ENT-REG-002, ENT-REG-004
  Rationale  : A host knows which services can be verified; no SQL or connection setting is exposed.
  Source     : POL-REG-013; ADR-REG-007
  Priority   : —

#### AC-REG-014 — [REQ-REG-013]
  Given  : 2 Service Packages are available and 1 is withdrawn
  When   : the list of services is requested
  Then   : the system returns 2 rows, each with serviceCode, available = true, versionNumber, fetchMode, documentType values and approvalEnabled, and no sqlText or connection field

### REQ-REG-014 — Read one service
  Pattern    : event
  Statement  : When a Host System or the Service Administrator requests a service by its service code, the system shall return the current version summary of that Service Package, including whether the Service Package is available or withdrawn.
  Traces     : US-REG-002, US-REG-007
  Entities   : ENT-REG-001, ENT-REG-002, ENT-REG-004
  Rationale  : The employee frontend and hosts see the required documents and fetch mode of a service, and whether new Checks may use it (ADR-REG-016). The read is case-insensitive on the service code (REQ-REG-064).
  Source     : POL-REG-008; ADR-REG-007; ADR-REG-016
  Priority   : —

#### AC-REG-015 — [REQ-REG-014]
  Given  : Service Package `scholarship-request` is available with current version 3
  When   : the service `scholarship-request` is requested
  Then   : the system returns serviceCode `scholarship-request`, available = true, versionNumber 3, fetchMode `path`, documentType values TRANSCRIPT and ID_CARD, and approvalEnabled = false

#### AC-REG-081 — [REQ-REG-014]
  Given  : Service Package `vehicle-permit` is withdrawn and its stored current version is 2
  When   : the service `vehicle-permit` is requested
  Then   : the system returns status 200 with serviceCode `vehicle-permit`, available = false and versionNumber 2

### REQ-REG-015 — Unknown service on read
  Pattern    : unwanted
  Statement  : If a Host System or the Service Administrator requests a service code that the registry does not hold, then the system shall return a not-found error.
  Traces     : US-REG-002
  Entities   : ENT-REG-001
  Rationale  : A read never invents a service.
  Source     : POL-REG-013; RULE-REG-016
  Priority   : —
  Rules      : applies RULE-REG-016

#### AC-REG-016 — [REQ-REG-015]
  Given  : the registry holds no service code `unknown-service`
  When   : the service `unknown-service` is requested
  Then   : the system returns status 404 with the RULE-REG-016 message

### REQ-REG-016 — Packages only from the package directory
  Pattern    : ubiquitous
  Statement  : The system shall accept service knowledge and service definitions only from the configured package directory that the Service Administrator maintains.
  Traces     : US-REG-003
  Entities   : ENT-REG-002
  Rationale  : Employees never supply service knowledge.
  Source     : POL-REG-004; [KB:raw-idea.md §2]; ADR-REG-007
  Priority   : —

#### AC-REG-017 — [REQ-REG-016]
  Given  : a valid package folder exists outside the configured package directory
  When   : the service starts
  Then   : the registry stores no version from that folder and the load report contains no row for it

### REQ-REG-017 — No write operation for employees or hosts
  Pattern    : ubiquitous
  Statement  : The system shall expose no operation through which an Employee or a Host System creates or changes a service package or a connection.
  Traces     : US-REG-003
  Entities   : ENT-REG-001, ENT-REG-002, ENT-REG-005
  Rationale  : Configuration is the Service Administrator's alone.
  Source     : POL-REG-004; ADR-REG-007
  Priority   : —

#### AC-REG-018 — [REQ-REG-017]
  Given  : the service is running
  When   : the operations of the service registry are listed
  Then   : every listed operation is a read operation and 0 operations create or change a Service Package, a version or a Connection

### REQ-REG-018 — Service knowledge supplied apart from data
  Pattern    : ubiquitous
  Statement  : The system shall supply the service knowledge to a Check as a separate part from every query and from every document reference, so that the service knowledge is the only service instruction text a Check gives the LLM.
  Traces     : US-REG-003, US-REG-005
  Entities   : ENT-REG-002
  Rationale  : Raw idea §12: document content is data, never instructions to the model.
  Source     : [KB:raw-idea.md §12]; ADR-REG-008
  Priority   : HIGH

#### AC-REG-019 — [REQ-REG-018]
  Given  : version 3 of `scholarship-request` is current
  When   : a Check requests the service package
  Then   : the supplied package returns the service knowledge in its own field, and that field contains 0 query texts and 0 document references

### REQ-REG-019 — Version number on every version
  Pattern    : ubiquitous
  Statement  : The system shall record a version number on every service package version.
  Traces     : US-REG-004
  Entities   : ENT-REG-002
  Rationale  : Every report records the version it was built on.
  Source     : POL-REG-005; G11
  Priority   : —

#### AC-REG-020 — [REQ-REG-019]
  Given  : a valid folder declares version 3
  When   : the package is loaded
  Then   : the stored version has versionNumber = 3

### REQ-REG-020 — A higher version becomes current
  Pattern    : event
  Statement  : When a loaded service package declares a version number higher than the current version of its service, the system shall register it as a new version and make it the current version.
  Traces     : US-REG-004
  Entities   : ENT-REG-002
  Rationale  : A change is always a new version.
  Source     : POL-REG-005; ADR-REG-003
  Priority   : —

#### AC-REG-021 — [REQ-REG-020]
  Given  : `scholarship-request` has current version 3
  When   : the service starts with the folder declaring version 4
  Then   : the registry stores version 4, version 4 becomes current, version 3 stays stored, and the load report records outcome REGISTERED

### REQ-REG-021 — Same version number with changed content rejected
  Pattern    : unwanted
  Statement  : If a loaded service package declares a version number already stored for its service with different content, then the system shall reject the package and keep the stored version unchanged.
  Traces     : US-REG-004
  Entities   : ENT-REG-002, ENT-REG-006
  Rationale  : A version is never edited in place.
  Source     : ADR-REG-003; RULE-REG-003
  Priority   : —
  Rules      : applies RULE-REG-003

#### AC-REG-022 — [REQ-REG-021]
  Given  : version 3 of `scholarship-request` is stored with contentHash `h1`
  When   : the service starts with a folder declaring version 3 whose contentHash is `h2`
  Then   : the package is rejected with the RULE-REG-003 message and version 3 keeps contentHash `h1`

### REQ-REG-022 — Lower unstored version rejected
  Pattern    : unwanted
  Statement  : If a loaded service package declares a version number lower than the current version of its service and not stored for it, then the system shall reject the package.
  Traces     : US-REG-004
  Entities   : ENT-REG-002, ENT-REG-006
  Rationale  : Versions only move forward.
  Source     : ADR-REG-003; RULE-REG-004
  Priority   : —
  Rules      : applies RULE-REG-004

#### AC-REG-023 — [REQ-REG-022]
  Given  : `scholarship-request` has current version 3 and no stored version 2
  When   : the service starts with the folder declaring version 2
  Then   : the package is rejected with the RULE-REG-004 message and version 3 stays current

### REQ-REG-023 — Unchanged package changes nothing
  Pattern    : event
  Statement  : When a loaded service package declares a stored version number with identical content, the system shall keep the stored version and register no new version.
  Traces     : US-REG-004
  Entities   : ENT-REG-002, ENT-REG-006
  Rationale  : A restart with unchanged folders leaves the registry as it was.
  Source     : ADR-REG-003
  Priority   : —

#### AC-REG-024 — [REQ-REG-023]
  Given  : version 3 of `scholarship-request` is stored with contentHash `h1`
  When   : the service starts with the folder declaring version 3 with contentHash `h1`
  Then   : the registry stores 0 new versions and the load report records outcome UNCHANGED

### REQ-REG-024 — Current version supplied to a Check
  Pattern    : event
  Statement  : When a Check requests the service package of an available service code, the system shall supply the current version with its version number.
  Traces     : US-REG-004
  Entities   : ENT-REG-002, ENT-REG-003, ENT-REG-004
  Rationale  : The Check and its report record the version used.
  Source     : POL-REG-005; ADR-REG-003
  Priority   : —

#### AC-REG-025 — [REQ-REG-024]
  Given  : `scholarship-request` has stored versions 2 and 3, version 3 current
  When   : a Check requests the service package of `scholarship-request`
  Then   : the system supplies version 3 and versionNumber = 3

### REQ-REG-025 — Resolve any stored version
  Pattern    : event
  Statement  : When a version is requested by service code and version number, the system shall return the service knowledge, service definition, queries and required document types of that version.
  Traces     : US-REG-004
  Entities   : ENT-REG-002, ENT-REG-003, ENT-REG-004
  Rationale  : A stored report traces back to the exact configuration it used.
  Source     : ADR-REG-003; G11
  Priority   : —

#### AC-REG-026 — [REQ-REG-025]
  Given  : `scholarship-request` version 2 is stored and version 3 is current
  When   : version 2 of `scholarship-request` is requested
  Then   : the system returns version 2 with its serviceKnowledge, serviceDefinition, queries and documentType values as stored

### REQ-REG-026 — No stored version is deleted
  Pattern    : ubiquitous
  Statement  : The system shall delete no stored service package version, no query and no required document of a stored version, and no Service Package.
  Traces     : US-REG-004
  Entities   : ENT-REG-001, ENT-REG-002, ENT-REG-003, ENT-REG-004
  Rationale  : Every loaded version stays resolvable.
  Source     : ADR-REG-003
  Priority   : —

#### AC-REG-027 — [REQ-REG-026]
  Given  : `vehicle-permit` with stored versions 1 and 2 is withdrawn
  When   : the service starts
  Then   : versions 1 and 2 of `vehicle-permit` still exist

### REQ-REG-027 — Whole service knowledge supplied
  Pattern    : event
  Statement  : When a Check requests the service package, the system shall supply the whole service knowledge text of the version, unaltered.
  Traces     : US-REG-005
  Entities   : ENT-REG-002
  Rationale  : No condition is missed through partial retrieval.
  Source     : POL-REG-006; [KB:raw-idea.md §2]
  Priority   : —

#### AC-REG-028 — [REQ-REG-027]
  Given  : the knowledge file of version 3 has 12000 characters
  When   : a Check requests the service package
  Then   : the supplied service knowledge has 12000 characters and equals the stored serviceKnowledge

### REQ-REG-028 — Empty service knowledge rejected
  Pattern    : unwanted
  Statement  : If the service knowledge file of a package is empty, then the system shall reject the package.
  Traces     : US-REG-005
  Entities   : ENT-REG-002, ENT-REG-006
  Rationale  : A service without conditions cannot be verified.
  Source     : POL-REG-006; RULE-REG-018
  Priority   : —
  Rules      : applies RULE-REG-018

#### AC-REG-029 — [REQ-REG-028]
  Given  : a package folder whose knowledge file has 0 characters
  When   : the service starts
  Then   : the package is rejected with the RULE-REG-018 message

### REQ-REG-029 — Only the queries of the service definition
  Pattern    : ubiquitous
  Statement  : The system shall supply to a Check only the queries written in the service definition of the version it uses, each with its SQL text unaltered and its connection name.
  Traces     : US-REG-006
  Entities   : ENT-REG-003
  Rationale  : No query is invented, altered or built from free text.
  Source     : POL-REG-007; [KB:raw-idea.md §4, §6, §12]
  Priority   : HIGH

#### AC-REG-030 — [REQ-REG-029]
  Given  : version 3 of `scholarship-request` defines queries `request_details` and `attachments`
  When   : a Check requests the service package
  Then   : the system supplies exactly 2 queries, each sqlText equal to the stored text and each with its connectionName

### REQ-REG-030 — Queries supplied only through the query channel
  Pattern    : ubiquitous
  Statement  : The system shall supply the queries of a service definition only through the query part of the supplied package, apart from the service knowledge.
  Traces     : US-REG-001, US-REG-006
  Entities   : ENT-REG-002, ENT-REG-003
  Rationale  : Raw idea §12: the LLM never writes SQL; it receives no query.
  Source     : [KB:raw-idea.md §6, §12]; G1; ADR-REG-008
  Priority   : HIGH

#### AC-REG-031 — [REQ-REG-030]
  Given  : version 3 of `scholarship-request` is current
  When   : a Check requests the service package
  Then   : the query part returns 2 queries and the serviceKnowledge field contains 0 SQL texts

  Pattern    : unwanted
  Statement  : If a query of a service definition names a connection that is not registered in the environment, then the system shall reject the package.
  Traces     : US-REG-006, US-REG-009
  Entities   : ENT-REG-003, ENT-REG-005, ENT-REG-006
  Rationale  : Every query runs against a known, read-only connection.
  Source     : POL-REG-007, POL-REG-010; RULE-REG-005
  Priority   : —
  Rules      : applies RULE-REG-005
