### REQ-REG-032 — Only named bind parameters of the declared input
  Pattern    : unwanted
  Statement  : If the SQL text of a query uses a parameter other than the declared input or a substitution marker other than a named bind parameter, then the system shall reject the package.
  Traces     : US-REG-006
  Entities   : ENT-REG-002, ENT-REG-003, ENT-REG-006
  Rationale  : Raw idea §12: parameters are bound; SQL is never built from free text.
  Source     : POL-REG-007; G4; ADR-REG-008; RULE-REG-006
  Priority   : HIGH
  Rules      : applies RULE-REG-006

#### AC-REG-033 — [REQ-REG-032]
  Given  : a service definition declares input `requestId`
  When   : a query contains `:studentId`

#### AC-REG-034 — [REQ-REG-032]

  When   : a query contains `${requestId}`
  Then   : the package is rejected with the RULE-REG-006 message

### REQ-REG-033 — Only single SELECT statements
  Pattern    : unwanted
  Statement  : If the SQL text of a query is not a single SELECT statement, then the system shall reject the package.
  Traces     : US-REG-006, US-REG-011
  Entities   : ENT-REG-003, ENT-REG-006
  Rationale  : Defence in depth beside the read-only database user.
  Source     : POL-REG-012; G3; ADR-REG-008; RULE-REG-007
  Priority   : HIGH
  Rules      : applies RULE-REG-007

#### AC-REG-035 — [REQ-REG-033]

### REQ-REG-034 — No per-service check limits
  Pattern    : unwanted
  Statement  : If a service definition declares a timeout, a maximum row count or a maximum file size, then the system shall reject the package.
  Traces     : US-REG-006
  Entities   : ENT-REG-002, ENT-REG-006

  Source     : ADR-REG-006; ADR-REG-008; RULE-REG-012
  Priority   : HIGH
  Rules      : applies RULE-REG-012

#### AC-REG-036 — [REQ-REG-034]
  Given  : a folder whose service definition declares `max_rows: 5000`

### REQ-REG-035 — Unique query names
  Pattern    : unwanted
  Statement  : If two queries of one service definition carry the same query name, then the system shall reject the package.
  Traces     : US-REG-006
  Entities   : ENT-REG-003, ENT-REG-006
  Rationale  : Each query is addressed by its name.
  Source     : POL-REG-007; RULE-REG-019
  Priority   : —
  Rules      : applies RULE-REG-019

  When   : the service starts
  Then   : the package is rejected with the RULE-REG-019 message

### REQ-REG-036 — One fetch mode per version
  Pattern    : ubiquitous
  Statement  : The system shall record one fetch mode — `path`, `blob` or `manual` — on every service package version.
  Traces     : US-REG-007
  Entities   : ENT-REG-002
  Rationale  : All three fetch modes are available, chosen per service.
  Source     : POL-REG-008; ADR-REG-005
  Priority   : —

#### AC-REG-038 — [REQ-REG-036]
  Given  : a valid folder whose service definition declares `fetch: path`
  When   : the package is loaded
  Then   : the stored version has fetchMode = path

### REQ-REG-037 — Required document types per version
  Pattern    : ubiquitous
  Statement  : The system shall record the required document types of every service package version.
  Traces     : US-REG-007
  Entities   : ENT-REG-004
  Rationale  : The required documents drive the deterministic check. A version may declare zero required document types (e.g. a `manual` service that only accepts uploads); each declared type appears once (REQ-REG-063).
  Source     : POL-REG-008
  Priority   : —

  Pattern    : unwanted
  Statement  : If a service definition declares a fetch mode other than `path`, `blob` or `manual`, then the system shall reject the package.
  Traces     : US-REG-007
  Entities   : ENT-REG-002, ENT-REG-006
  Rationale  : Fetch mode is a closed enum.
  Source     : ADR-REG-005; RULE-REG-008
  Priority   : —

  Pattern    : unwanted

  Pattern    : unwanted

  Entities   : ENT-REG-002, ENT-REG-003, ENT-REG-005, ENT-REG-006

### REQ-REG-041 — No file location in a service package
  Pattern    : ubiquitous
  Statement  : The system shall take document locations only from the document location column of the document source query and store no file location in a service package.
  Traces     : US-REG-007
  Entities   : ENT-REG-002
  Rationale  : Raw idea §12: file paths come from host data and are validated against the storage root before opening.

  Priority   : HIGH
  Rules      : applies RULE-REG-012

  Entities   : ENT-REG-002

  Pattern    : unwanted

  Entities   : ENT-REG-002, ENT-REG-006

  Entities   : ENT-REG-002
  Rationale  : Raw idea §12: approval is executed only as a result of the employee's action.

  Entities   : ENT-REG-002

  Pattern    : ubiquitous

  Entities   : ENT-REG-005

  Entities   : ENT-REG-005

### REQ-REG-049 — Activation registers the environment's connections
  Pattern    : event

  Entities   : ENT-REG-005, ENT-REG-006

  Priority   : —

#### AC-REG-051 — [REQ-REG-049]
  Given  : the activation configuration of environment `test` lists `main-db` and `blob-db`
  When   : the service starts in `test`
  Then   : the registry stores 2 Connections with environmentName `test` and the load report records 2 ACTIVATED rows

### REQ-REG-050 — Changed connection settings updated
  Pattern    : event
  Statement  : When the activation configuration changes the settings of a registered connection, the system shall update that Connection and leave every service package version unchanged.
  Traces     : US-REG-010
  Entities   : ENT-REG-005, ENT-REG-002
  Rationale  : Only the connections differ between environments and over time.
  Source     : POL-REG-011; ADR-REG-009
  Priority   : —

#### AC-REG-052 — [REQ-REG-050]
  Given  : Connection `main-db` is registered with endpoint `e1`
  When   : the service starts with `main-db` at endpoint `e2`
  Then   : the registry sets endpoint = `e2` on `main-db`, records outcome UPDATED, and stores 0 new service package versions

### REQ-REG-051 — Connection no longer listed removed
  Pattern    : event
  Statement  : When a registered connection is absent from the activation configuration, the system shall remove that Connection.
  Traces     : US-REG-010
  Entities   : ENT-REG-005, ENT-REG-006
  Rationale  : The registry holds only the connections the environment declares.
  Source     : POL-REG-011; ADR-REG-009
  Priority   : —

#### AC-REG-053 — [REQ-REG-051]
  Given  : Connection `archive-db` is registered and absent from the activation configuration
  When   : the service starts
  Then   : the registry deletes `archive-db` and the load report records outcome REMOVED

### REQ-REG-052 — Unknown connection type refused
  Pattern    : unwanted
  Statement  : If the activation configuration lists a connection whose type is neither `mcp` nor `jdbc`, then the system shall refuse that connection.
  Traces     : US-REG-010
  Entities   : ENT-REG-005, ENT-REG-006
  Rationale  : Connection type is a closed list.
  Source     : ADR-REG-004; RULE-REG-014
  Priority   : —
  Rules      : applies RULE-REG-014

#### AC-REG-054 — [REQ-REG-052]
  Given  : the activation configuration lists `ftp-db` of type `ftp`
  When   : the service starts
  Then   : `ftp-db` is not registered and the load report records REJECTED with the RULE-REG-014 message

### REQ-REG-053 — Version naming an unregistered connection not supplied
  Pattern    : unwanted
  Statement  : If the current version of a service names in a query a connection that the environment does not register, then the system shall refuse to supply that version to a Check.
  Traces     : US-REG-010
  Entities   : ENT-REG-003, ENT-REG-005
  Rationale  : A Check never starts against a data source that is not activated.
  Source     : ADR-REG-009; RULE-REG-017
  Priority   : —
  Rules      : applies RULE-REG-017

#### AC-REG-055 — [REQ-REG-053]
  Given  : the current version of `scholarship-request` names `main-db` and `main-db` was removed at activation
  When   : a Check requests the service package of `scholarship-request`
  Then   : the system supplies no package and returns the RULE-REG-017 message

### REQ-REG-054 — Credential referenced, never stored
  Pattern    : ubiquitous
  Statement  : The system shall store for each Connection the reference of its credential in the environment's secret store and never the credential itself.
  Traces     : US-REG-009, US-REG-011
  Entities   : ENT-REG-005
  Rationale  : Host-data credentials stay in one protected place.
  Source     : ADR-REG-009
  Priority   : —

#### AC-REG-056 — [REQ-REG-054]
  Given  : the activation configuration lists `main-db` with credentialReference `aias/main-db`
  When   : the service starts
  Then   : the stored Connection has credentialReference = `aias/main-db` and 0 stored fields contain the password

### REQ-REG-055 — Connection without a read-only declaration refused
  Pattern    : unwanted
  Statement  : If a connection in the activation configuration is not declared read-only, then the system shall refuse to activate that connection.
  Traces     : US-REG-011
  Entities   : ENT-REG-005, ENT-REG-006
  Rationale  : Raw idea §12: all access to host data uses a read-only database user.
  Source     : POL-REG-012; G3; ADR-REG-008; ADR-REG-009; RULE-REG-015
  Priority   : HIGH
  Rules      : applies RULE-REG-015

#### AC-REG-057 — [REQ-REG-055]
  Given  : the activation configuration lists `main-db` with readOnly = false
  When   : the service starts
  Then   : `main-db` is not registered and the load report records REJECTED with the RULE-REG-015 message

### REQ-REG-056 — Limited-to-views declaration recorded
  Pattern    : ubiquitous
  Statement  : The system shall record on every Connection whether its database user is limited to specific views.
  Traces     : US-REG-011
  Entities   : ENT-REG-005
  Rationale  : The read-only user is preferably limited to specific views.
  Source     : POL-REG-012; [KB:raw-idea.md §12]
  Priority   : —

#### AC-REG-058 — [REQ-REG-056]
  Given  : the activation configuration lists `main-db` with limitedToViews = true
  When   : the service starts
  Then   : the stored Connection `main-db` has limitedToViews = true

### REQ-REG-057 — Scholarship request pilot package
  Pattern    : ubiquitous
  Statement  : The system shall provide the service package `scholarship-request` with fetch mode `path` and the required document types TRANSCRIPT and ID_CARD.
  Traces     : US-REG-012
  Entities   : ENT-REG-001, ENT-REG-002, ENT-REG-004
  Rationale  : The pilot proves the generic model on clear numeric conditions.
  Source     : POL-REG-014; D5
  Priority   : HIGH

#### AC-REG-059 — [REQ-REG-057]
  Given  : a fresh deployment with the delivered package directory and connection `main-db` activated
  When   : the service starts
  Then   : the registry returns Service Package `scholarship-request` with fetchMode = path and documentType values TRANSCRIPT and ID_CARD

### REQ-REG-058 — Pilot numeric conditions explicit
  Pattern    : ubiquitous
  Statement  : The system shall provide the service knowledge of `scholarship-request` with every numeric condition stated as an explicit number.
  Traces     : US-REG-012
  Entities   : ENT-REG-002
  Rationale  : Explicit numbers make the pilot's findings verifiable.
  Source     : POL-REG-014; D5
  Priority   : HIGH

#### AC-REG-060 — [REQ-REG-058]
  Given  : the delivered service knowledge of `scholarship-request`
  When   : it is read
  Then   : each numeric condition contains its threshold as digits and 0 numeric conditions state their threshold in words

### REQ-REG-059 — No request data in the registry
  Pattern    : ubiquitous
  Statement  : The system shall store no request data — no request number, employee identity, query result or document content — in the service registry.
  Traces     : US-REG-013
  Entities   : ENT-REG-001, ENT-REG-002, ENT-REG-003, ENT-REG-004, ENT-REG-005, ENT-REG-006
  Rationale  : Raw idea §12: no data is carried from one Check to another.
  Source     : POL-REG-015; G9; ADR-REG-008
  Priority   : HIGH

#### AC-REG-061 — [REQ-REG-059]
  Given  : 100 Checks of `scholarship-request` have run
  When   : the service registry data is read
  Then   : 0 stored fields contain a request number, an employee identity, a query result or a document content

### REQ-REG-060 — Package content supplied read-only
  Pattern    : ubiquitous
  Statement  : The system shall supply service package content to every Check as read-only data that no Check can change.
  Traces     : US-REG-013
  Entities   : ENT-REG-002, ENT-REG-003, ENT-REG-004
  Rationale  : One Check cannot alter what the next Check receives.
  Source     : POL-REG-015; G9; ADR-REG-008
  Priority   : HIGH

#### AC-REG-062 — [REQ-REG-060]
  Given  : a Check received version 3 of `scholarship-request`
  When   : that Check attempts to change the supplied query text
  Then   : the change is refused and the next Check receives the stored sqlText unchanged

### REQ-REG-061 — Pilot package failure reported
  Pattern    : unwanted
  Statement  : If the `scholarship-request` package fails validation at start, then the system shall record outcome REJECTED for it in the load report and supply no `scholarship-request` package to Checks.
  Traces     : US-REG-012
  Entities   : ENT-REG-001, ENT-REG-006
  Rationale  : A broken pilot never runs silently on stale or partial configuration.
  Source     : POL-REG-014; [KB:raw-idea.md §12]
  Priority   : HIGH

#### AC-REG-063 — [REQ-REG-061]
  Given  : the delivered `scholarship-request` folder declares `fetch: fax` and no version of it is stored
  When   : the service starts
  Then   : the load report records outcome REJECTED for `scholarship-request` and a Check requesting it receives the RULE-REG-016 message

### REQ-REG-062 — Package folder holds only its two files
  Pattern    : unwanted
  Statement  : If a service package folder contains a file other than the service knowledge file and the service definition file, then the system shall reject the package.
  Traces     : US-REG-013
  Entities   : ENT-REG-002, ENT-REG-006
  Rationale  : No request data or document can enter the registry through a package folder.
  Source     : POL-REG-015; G9
  Priority   : HIGH
  Rules      : applies RULE-REG-020

#### AC-REG-064 — [REQ-REG-062]
  Given  : a package folder that also holds `request-4711.pdf`
  When   : the service starts
  Then   : the package is rejected with the RULE-REG-020 message

### REQ-REG-063 — Duplicate required document type rejected
  Pattern    : unwanted
  Statement  : If a service definition declares the same required document type more than once, then the system shall reject the package.
  Traces     : US-REG-007
  Entities   : ENT-REG-004, ENT-REG-006
  Rationale  : Each required document type is counted once by the deterministic check; a repeated type is a definition error, reported in the load report instead of failing the load on a database constraint.
  Source     : POL-REG-008; RULE-REG-021
  Priority   : —
  Rules      : applies RULE-REG-021

#### AC-REG-065 — [REQ-REG-063]
  Given  : a folder `demo-service` whose service definition declares `required: [TRANSCRIPT, ID_CARD, TRANSCRIPT]`
  When   : the service starts
  Then   : the package is rejected with the RULE-REG-021 message and no version is stored for it

### REQ-REG-064 — Service codes compared in canonical form
  Pattern    : ubiquitous
  Statement  : The system shall trim every service code it receives, compare service codes case-insensitively and store each service code in lower case.
  Traces     : US-REG-001, US-REG-002
  Entities   : ENT-REG-001, ENT-REG-006
  Rationale  : One package per service needs one definition of "the same service code"; consumers never guess the casing (POL-REG-013).
  Source     : POL-REG-001, POL-REG-013; ADR-REG-017
  Priority   : —

#### AC-REG-066 — [REQ-REG-064]
  Given  : version 3 of `scholarship-request` is stored with contentHash `h1`
  When   : the service starts with the folder declaring service code ` Scholarship-Request ` and version 3 with contentHash `h1`
  Then   : the registry holds 1 Service Package `scholarship-request`, stores 0 new versions and the load report records outcome UNCHANGED

#### AC-REG-067 — [REQ-REG-064]
  Given  : folder `a` declares service code `Scholarship-Request` and folder `b` declares `scholarship-request`
  When   : the service starts
  Then   : both packages are rejected with the RULE-REG-002 message for service code `scholarship-request`

#### AC-REG-068 — [REQ-REG-064]
  Given  : Service Package `scholarship-request` is available
  When   : the service `SCHOLARSHIP-REQUEST` is requested
  Then   : the system returns status 200 with serviceCode `scholarship-request`

### REQ-REG-065 — Invalid service code rejected
  Pattern    : unwanted
  Statement  : If a service definition declares a service code that, trimmed and in lower case, is empty, longer than 100 characters or not made of letters a-z, digits and single hyphens between them, then the system shall reject the package.
  Traces     : US-REG-001, US-REG-002
  Entities   : ENT-REG-001, ENT-REG-006
  Rationale  : A service code is a stable key used in paths and stored reports.
  Source     : POL-REG-013; ADR-REG-017; RULE-REG-022
  Priority   : —
  Rules      : applies RULE-REG-022

#### AC-REG-069 — [REQ-REG-065]
  Given  : a folder whose service definition declares service code `scholarship request!`
  When   : the service starts
  Then   : the package is rejected with the RULE-REG-022 message and no Service Package is created

### REQ-REG-066 — Unreachable or empty package directory withdraws nothing
  Pattern    : unwanted
  Statement  : If the configured package directory does not exist or cannot be read, or holds no package folder while the registry holds at least one available Service Package, then the system shall load no package, withdraw no Service Package and record one rejected Load Result for the package directory.
  Traces     : US-REG-002
  Entities   : ENT-REG-001, ENT-REG-006
  Rationale  : A wrong path or a failed mount must not withdraw every service; the load report says why nothing was loaded (raw idea §12).
  Source     : [KB:raw-idea.md §12]; ADR-REG-018; RULE-REG-023
  Priority   : HIGH
  Rules      : applies RULE-REG-023

#### AC-REG-070 — [REQ-REG-066]
  Given  : the registry holds 2 available Service Packages and the configured package directory does not exist
  When   : the service starts
  Then   : both Service Packages stay available, 0 versions are stored, and the load report records 1 row with subjectKind PACKAGE_DIRECTORY, outcome REJECTED and the RULE-REG-023 message

#### AC-REG-071 — [REQ-REG-066]
  Given  : the registry holds 2 available Service Packages and the configured package directory exists but holds no folder
  When   : the service starts
  Then   : both Service Packages stay available and the load report records 1 row with subjectKind PACKAGE_DIRECTORY, outcome REJECTED and the RULE-REG-023 message

### REQ-REG-067 — One load run at a time
  Pattern    : ubiquitous
  Statement  : The system shall run each load run — Load Result replacement, connection activation, package loading and withdrawal — as one transaction that holds one exclusive database lock until it ends.
  Traces     : US-REG-002, US-REG-010
  Entities   : ENT-REG-005, ENT-REG-006
  Rationale  : Two instances starting together never interleave their load runs, so the load report is always the complete report of exactly one run.
  Source     : ADR-REG-015; ADR-REG-007
  Priority   : HIGH

#### AC-REG-072 — [REQ-REG-067]
  Given  : two instances of the service share one schema, and the package directory holds 2 valid folders and the activation configuration 1 connection
  When   : both instances start at the same moment
  Then   : the load report holds exactly 3 rows, all with one loadRunAt, and the registry holds each of the 2 versions once and 1 Connection

### REQ-REG-068 — Load lock not acquired in time
  Pattern    : unwanted
  Statement  : If the system cannot take the load lock within the configured load lock timeout, then the system shall run no load run and serve the registry as stored.
  Traces     : US-REG-002
  Entities   : ENT-REG-001, ENT-REG-006
  Rationale  : An instance blocked by another instance's load run starts on the committed registry instead of failing.
  Source     : ADR-REG-015
  Priority   : —

#### AC-REG-073 — [REQ-REG-068]
  Given  : another instance holds the load lock for longer than the load lock timeout of 30 seconds and the registry holds version 3 of `scholarship-request`
  When   : the service starts
  Then   : the starting instance writes 0 Load Result rows and supplies version 3 of `scholarship-request` to a Check

### REQ-REG-069 — Package changed while read rejected
  Pattern    : unwanted
  Statement  : If a file of a service package folder changes in size or modification time while the load run reads it, then the system shall reject the package.
  Traces     : US-REG-002, US-REG-004
  Entities   : ENT-REG-002, ENT-REG-006
  Rationale  : A half-written file must never be stored as a version or taken into its content hash.
  Source     : ADR-REG-003; ADR-REG-007; RULE-REG-024
  Priority   : —
  Rules      : applies RULE-REG-024

#### AC-REG-074 — [REQ-REG-069]
  Given  : the service definition file of folder `scholarship-request` grows from 2048 to 4096 bytes between the start and the end of its read
  When   : the service starts
  Then   : the package is rejected with the RULE-REG-024 message and no new version is stored

## A5 — Business rules

### RULE-REG-001 — Complete package
  Scope      : ENT-REG-002
  Trigger    : on load
  Statement  : The system shall reject a package when its folder lacks the service knowledge file or the service definition file.
  Message    : The service package "{folder}" is incomplete: it needs both its service knowledge and its service definition.
  Traces     : REQ-REG-003
  Data source: ENT-REG-002.serviceKnowledge, ENT-REG-002.serviceDefinition
  Source     : POL-REG-001, POL-REG-002

### RULE-REG-002 — One folder per service code
  Scope      : ENT-REG-001
  Trigger    : on load
  Statement  : The system shall reject every package that declares a service code when two or more folders declare that service code.
  Message    : The service code "{serviceCode}" is declared by more than one package folder; keep one folder per service.
  Traces     : REQ-REG-004
  Data source: ENT-REG-001.serviceCode
  Source     : POL-REG-001

### RULE-REG-003 — Versions are never edited in place
  Scope      : ENT-REG-002
  Trigger    : on load
  Statement  : The system shall reject a package when its version number is already stored for its service and its content hash differs from the stored one.
  Message    : Version {versionNumber} of "{serviceCode}" already exists with different content; publish the change as a new version.
  Traces     : REQ-REG-021
  Data source: ENT-REG-002.versionNumber, ENT-REG-002.contentHash
  Source     : ADR-REG-003

### RULE-REG-004 — Versions only move forward
  Scope      : ENT-REG-002
  Trigger    : on load
  Statement  : The system shall reject a package when its version number is lower than the current version and is not stored.
  Message    : Version {versionNumber} of "{serviceCode}" is older than the current version {currentVersion}.
  Traces     : REQ-REG-022
  Data source: ENT-REG-002.versionNumber
  Source     : ADR-REG-003

### RULE-REG-005 — Query connection registered
  Scope      : ENT-REG-003
  Trigger    : on load
  Statement  : The system shall reject a package when a query names a connection that the environment does not register.
  Message    : The query "{queryName}" uses the connection "{connectionName}", which is not activated in this environment.
  Traces     : REQ-REG-031
  Data source: ENT-REG-003.connectionName, ENT-REG-005.connectionName
  Source     : POL-REG-007, POL-REG-010

### RULE-REG-006 — Bound parameters only
  Scope      : ENT-REG-003
  Trigger    : on load
  Statement  : The system shall reject a package when a query uses a parameter other than the declared input or a substitution marker other than a named bind parameter.
  Message    : The query "{queryName}" uses "{marker}"; queries take only the bind parameter ":{inputName}".
  Traces     : REQ-REG-032
  Data source: ENT-REG-003.sqlText, ENT-REG-002.inputName
  Source     : POL-REG-007; G4
  Test-Hint  : Try `:other`, `${x}`, `#{x}` and string concatenation.

### RULE-REG-007 — Single SELECT statement
  Scope      : ENT-REG-003
  Trigger    : on load
  Statement  : The system shall reject a package when a query text holds anything other than one SELECT statement, with or without a leading WITH clause.
  Message    : The query "{queryName}" must be one SELECT statement; it cannot change data or run several statements.
  Traces     : REQ-REG-033
  Data source: ENT-REG-003.sqlText
  Source     : POL-REG-012; G3; ADR-REG-008

### RULE-REG-008 — Fetch mode closed
  Scope      : ENT-REG-002
  Trigger    : on load
  Statement  : The system shall reject a package when its fetch mode is not `path`, `blob` or `manual`.
  Message    : The fetch mode "{fetchMode}" is not supported; use path, blob or manual.
  Traces     : REQ-REG-038
  Data source: ENT-REG-002.fetchMode
  Source     : ADR-REG-005; profile conventions.lookups

### RULE-REG-009 — Document source complete
  Scope      : ENT-REG-002
  Trigger    : on load
  Statement  : The system shall reject a package when its fetch mode is `path` or `blob` and the document source query, the document type column or the document location column (path column for `path`, content column for `blob`) is missing, or the document source query is not a query of the same service definition.
  Message    : The documents of "{serviceCode}" cannot be fetched: the document source is incomplete.
  Traces     : REQ-REG-039
  Data source: ENT-REG-002.fetchMode, ENT-REG-002.documentSourceQueryName, ENT-REG-002.documentTypeColumn, ENT-REG-002.documentPathColumn, ENT-REG-002.documentContentColumn, ENT-REG-003.queryName
  Source     : POL-REG-008

### RULE-REG-010 — Blob over jdbc only
  Scope      : ENT-REG-002, ENT-REG-003
  Trigger    : on load
  Statement  : The system shall reject a package when its fetch mode is `blob` and the connection of its document source query is not of type `jdbc`.
  Message    : Documents stored in the database are read over a read-only jdbc connection; "{connectionName}" is not one.
  Traces     : REQ-REG-040
  Data source: ENT-REG-002.documentSourceQueryName, ENT-REG-003.connectionName, ENT-REG-005.connectionType
  Source     : G14; ADR-REG-004

### RULE-REG-011 — Approval definition required
  Scope      : ENT-REG-002
  Trigger    : on load
  Statement  : The system shall reject a package when the approval API is enabled and no approval API definition is given.
  Message    : The approval API of "{serviceCode}" is enabled but not defined.
  Traces     : REQ-REG-043
  Data source: ENT-REG-002.approvalEnabled, ENT-REG-002.approvalApi
  Source     : POL-REG-009

### RULE-REG-012 — Closed service definition structure
  Scope      : ENT-REG-002
  Trigger    : on load
  Statement  : The system shall reject a package when its service definition contains an element outside the service definition structure (service, version, input, queries, documents, approval), including any timeout, maximum row count, maximum file size or file location.
  Message    : The service definition of "{serviceCode}" contains "{element}", which a service definition cannot set.
  Traces     : REQ-REG-034, REQ-REG-041
  Data source: ENT-REG-002.serviceDefinition
  Source     : ADR-REG-006; ADR-REG-008

### RULE-REG-013 — Unique connection name
  Scope      : ENT-REG-005
  Trigger    : on activation
  Statement  : The system shall refuse every connection of a name when the activation configuration lists that name more than once.
  Message    : The connection name "{connectionName}" is listed more than once.
  Traces     : REQ-REG-047
  Data source: ENT-REG-005.connectionName
  Source     : POL-REG-010

### RULE-REG-014 — Connection type closed
  Scope      : ENT-REG-005
  Trigger    : on activation
  Statement  : The system shall refuse a connection when its type is neither `mcp` nor `jdbc`.
  Message    : The connection "{connectionName}" has the type "{connectionType}"; use mcp or jdbc.
  Traces     : REQ-REG-052
  Data source: ENT-REG-005.connectionType
  Source     : ADR-REG-004

### RULE-REG-015 — Read-only declaration required
  Scope      : ENT-REG-005
  Trigger    : on activation
  Statement  : The system shall refuse a connection when it is not declared read-only.
  Message    : The connection "{connectionName}" must use a read-only database user.
  Traces     : REQ-REG-055
  Data source: ENT-REG-005.readOnly
  Source     : POL-REG-012; G3; ADR-REG-009

### RULE-REG-016 — Withdrawn or unknown service not supplied
  Scope      : ENT-REG-001
  Trigger    : on package request and on service read
  Statement  : The system shall refuse to supply or return a service when its service code is not held by the registry or its Service Package is withdrawn (for a read, only an unknown code is refused).
  Message    : The service "{serviceCode}" is not available.
  Traces     : REQ-REG-012, REQ-REG-015
  Data source: ENT-REG-001.serviceCode, ENT-REG-001.available
  Source     : POL-REG-013; ARCH-5 (effect on SOFT-READ consumers: prevent new Checks; stored versions stay resolvable)

### RULE-REG-017 — Current version needs activated connections
  Scope      : ENT-REG-003
  Trigger    : on package request
  Statement  : The system shall refuse to supply the current version of a service when one of its queries names a connection the environment does not register.
  Message    : The service "{serviceCode}" uses the connection "{connectionName}", which is not activated in this environment.
  Traces     : REQ-REG-053
  Data source: ENT-REG-003.connectionName, ENT-REG-005.connectionName
  Source     : ADR-REG-009; ARCH-5 (effect of a removed Connection on consumers: prevent)

### RULE-REG-018 — Service knowledge not empty
  Scope      : ENT-REG-002
  Trigger    : on load
  Statement  : The system shall reject a package when its service knowledge text is empty.
  Message    : The service knowledge of "{serviceCode}" is empty.
  Traces     : REQ-REG-028
  Data source: ENT-REG-002.serviceKnowledge
  Source     : POL-REG-006

### RULE-REG-019 — Unique query names
  Scope      : ENT-REG-003
  Trigger    : on load
  Statement  : The system shall reject a package when two queries of its service definition carry the same query name.
  Message    : The query name "{queryName}" is used twice in "{serviceCode}".
  Traces     : REQ-REG-035
  Data source: ENT-REG-003.queryName
  Source     : POL-REG-007

### RULE-REG-020 — Package folder content closed
  Scope      : ENT-REG-002
  Trigger    : on load
  Statement  : The system shall reject a package when its folder holds any file besides the service knowledge file and the service definition file.
  Message    : The package folder "{folder}" holds "{file}"; a package folder holds only its service knowledge and its service definition.
  Traces     : REQ-REG-062
  Data source: ENT-REG-002.serviceKnowledge, ENT-REG-002.serviceDefinition
  Source     : POL-REG-015; G9

### RULE-REG-021 — Unique required document types
  Scope      : ENT-REG-004
  Trigger    : on load
  Statement  : The system shall reject a package when its service definition declares the same required document type more than once.
  Message    : The document type "{documentType}" is required more than once in "{serviceCode}".
  Traces     : REQ-REG-063
  Data source: ENT-REG-004.documentType
  Source     : POL-REG-008

### RULE-REG-022 — Valid service code
  Scope      : ENT-REG-001
  Trigger    : on load
  Statement  : The system shall reject a package when its declared service code, trimmed and in lower case, is empty, longer than 100 characters or not made of letters a-z, digits and single hyphens between them.
  Message    : The service code "{serviceCode}" is not valid; use lower-case letters, digits and single hyphens, at most 100 characters.
  Traces     : REQ-REG-065
  Data source: ENT-REG-001.serviceCode
  Source     : POL-REG-013; ADR-REG-017
  Test-Hint  : Try a space, `_`, a leading or double hyphen and a 101-character code.

### RULE-REG-023 — Package directory reachable
  Scope      : ENT-REG-006
  Trigger    : on load
  Statement  : The system shall load no package and withdraw no Service Package when the package directory does not exist, cannot be read, or holds no package folder while at least one Service Package is available.
  Message    : The package directory "{directory}" is missing, unreadable or empty; no service was loaded or withdrawn.
  Traces     : REQ-REG-066
  Data source: ENT-REG-001.available, ENT-REG-006.subjectName
  Source     : [KB:raw-idea.md §12]; ADR-REG-018

### RULE-REG-024 — Stable package read
  Scope      : ENT-REG-002
  Trigger    : on load
  Statement  : The system shall reject a package when the size or modification time of one of its files differs between the start and the end of its read.
  Message    : The package folder "{folder}" changed while it was being read; publish it again and restart.
  Traces     : REQ-REG-069
  Data source: ENT-REG-002.serviceKnowledge, ENT-REG-002.serviceDefinition, ENT-REG-002.contentHash
  Source     : ADR-REG-003; ADR-REG-007

## A6 — Lookups

```yaml name=lookups
lookups:
  - {key: SERVICE_CODE, seeded: [scholarship-request], open: true, values: [scholarship-request], fields: [serviceCode], entity: ENT-REG-001, control: reference, source: "POL-REG-013, POL-REG-014; profile conventions.lookups — added as data by each package folder"}
  - {key: CONNECTION_TYPE, seeded: [mcp, jdbc], open: false, values: [mcp, jdbc], fields: [connectionType], entity: ENT-REG-005, control: lookup, source: "[KB:raw-idea.md §4, §6]; ADR-REG-004"}
  - {key: DOCUMENT_TYPE, seeded: [TRANSCRIPT, ID_CARD], open: true, values: [TRANSCRIPT, ID_CARD], fields: [documentType], entity: ENT-REG-004, control: lookup, source: "POL-REG-014 custom lookup values; further values added as data by each service definition"}
  - {key: LOAD_OUTCOME, seeded: [REGISTERED, UNCHANGED, REJECTED, WITHDRAWN, ACTIVATED, UPDATED, REMOVED], open: false, values: [REGISTERED, UNCHANGED, REJECTED, WITHDRAWN, ACTIVATED, UPDATED, REMOVED], fields: [outcome], entity: ENT-REG-006, control: lookup, source: "ADR-REG-007"}
  - {key: LOAD_SUBJECT, seeded: [SERVICE_PACKAGE, CONNECTION, PACKAGE_DIRECTORY], open: false, values: [SERVICE_PACKAGE, CONNECTION, PACKAGE_DIRECTORY], fields: [subjectKind], entity: ENT-REG-006, control: lookup, source: "ADR-REG-007; ADR-REG-018"}
  - {key: FETCH_MODE, seeded: [], open: false, values: [path, blob, manual], fields: [fetchMode], owner: DOC, entity: ENT-REG-002, control: lookup, source: "profile conventions.lookups closed enum; consumed by value with no runtime read of DOC (ADR-REG-005)"}
```

| Key | Labels (en) | Rationale |
|---|---|---|
| SERVICE_CODE | the code itself | Growing set → reference to ENT-REG-001; never hardcoded |
| CONNECTION_TYPE | MCP server · JDBC (read-only, `blob` documents) | Closed: two channels exist (ADR-REG-004) |
| DOCUMENT_TYPE | Transcript · ID card | Open: each service names the host's document types |
| LOAD_OUTCOME | Registered · Unchanged · Rejected · Withdrawn · Activated · Updated · Removed | Closed: every load outcome |
| LOAD_SUBJECT | Service package · Connection · Package directory | Closed (PACKAGE_DIRECTORY — ADR-REG-018) |
| FETCH_MODE | Path · Blob · Manual | Consumed (owner DOC by vocabulary; profile closed enum) |

## A7 — Status lifecycle
Not applicable: no REG entity has more than 2 states. Service Package: available ⇄ withdrawn (flag `available`, REQ-REG-010, REQ-REG-011). Versions are immutable (ADR-REG-003).

## A8 — Module dependencies
```yaml name=module-dependencies
consumes: []
```
REG is the root of the platform graph (tier 0); it consumes no entity of another module.

| External service | Purpose | Integration kind |
|---|---|---|
| Package directory (environment file storage) | Source of the service package folders | file read at start-up (ADR-REG-007) |
| Activation configuration and secret store of the environment | Source of connections; holds the credentials REG only references | configuration read at start-up (ADR-REG-009) |
| Oracle SQLcl MCP server / read-only JDBC data sources | Named by connections; called by CHK and DOC, never by REG | none from REG |

# PART B — SCREEN REQUIREMENTS

Not applicable: REG has no screen in this version. The full administration UI is out of scope (scope exceptions, AIAS-2); the Service Administrator maintains package folders and the activation configuration directly (ADR-REG-007). The employee frontend's screens belong to INT/RPT.

## API expectations (module level — no screen carries them)
Base path : /api/v1/{resource}
Verbs     : GET = read (REG exposes no create, update or delete — ADR-REG-007)
Errors    : ProblemDetail (RFC 9457) → {type, title, status, detail, code}

| Operation | Verb | Path (per base path) | Inputs | Outputs | RULEs | Traces (REQ) |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| list services | GET | /api/v1/services | — | list of current version summaries | — | REQ-REG-013 |
| read service | GET | /api/v1/services/{serviceCode} | serviceCode (trimmed, case-insensitive — REQ-REG-064) | current version summary | RULE-REG-016 | REQ-REG-014, REQ-REG-015, REQ-REG-064 |
| read load report | GET | /api/v1/load-results | — | Load Result rows of the latest run | — | REQ-REG-008 |
| supply service package (in-process, REG interface) | — | — | serviceCode | current version: knowledge, queries, document settings | RULE-REG-016, RULE-REG-017 | REQ-REG-024, REQ-REG-027, REQ-REG-029, REQ-REG-030 |
| resolve version (in-process) | — | — | serviceCode, versionNumber | full version | — | REQ-REG-025 |
| supply connection (in-process) | — | — | connectionName | connection settings | — | REQ-REG-048 |
| supply approval API (in-process, Employee Decision path only) | — | — | serviceCode, versionNumber | approvalEnabled, approvalApi | — | REQ-REG-044, REQ-REG-045 |

A version summary carries serviceCode, available, versionNumber, fetchMode, documentType values and approvalEnabled — never SQL text or connection settings (`available` — ADR-REG-016).

## Screens

None. REG v1 owns no screen, so this spec carries no screen block, no `Composition` line and no cross-module display dependency.

| Source | Statement |
|---|---|
| SRS PART B | "Not applicable: REG has no screen in this version. The full administration UI is out of scope (scope exceptions, AIAS-2) … The employee frontend's screens belong to INT/RPT." |
| SRS Access summary | Service Administrator: screens none (no administration UI); Employee / Host System: none in REG |
| Registry P1 | Screens: none — no SCR-REQ in this version |
| Raw idea §2, §15 A1 | A full administration UI stays out of scope; the employee frontend is a web frontend embedded in the host screen, owned by INT |
| Raw idea §15 A2 | Caller authentication and the security phases are deferred — no permission is shown or checked on any screen |

## Cross-module display dependencies

None minted here. A `UXD-*` is keyed by the module that owns the **screen** (engine A.5): when an INT screen renders a REG field (a service's fetch mode or required document types, from API-REG-001 or API-REG-002), INT mints and records that UXD in its own spec and registry.

## Fields REG's read API exposes to a consuming screen

For reference only — no REG screen renders them. Shapes are read in `api-spec-reg.yaml` (`ServiceSummary`, `LoadResult`), not restated beyond the labels the SRS gives.

| Field | Operation | Label (en) | Label (ar) |
|---|---|---|---|
| serviceCode | API-REG-001, API-REG-002, API-REG-003 | Service code | PENDING ADR-REG-011 |
| available | API-REG-001, API-REG-002 | Available (false when the service is withdrawn — ADR-REG-016) | PENDING ADR-REG-011 |
| versionNumber | API-REG-001, API-REG-002, API-REG-003 | Version | PENDING ADR-REG-011 |
| fetchMode | API-REG-001, API-REG-002 | Fetch mode | PENDING ADR-REG-011 |
| requiredDocumentTypes | API-REG-001, API-REG-002 | Document type | PENDING ADR-REG-011 |
| approvalEnabled | API-REG-001, API-REG-002 | Approval API enabled | PENDING ADR-REG-011 |
| subjectKind, subjectName, outcome, reason, loadRunAt | API-REG-003 | Subject kind, Subject, Outcome, Reason, Load run | PENDING ADR-REG-011 |

Service codes in every response are canonical lower case; a read matches the code trimmed and case-insensitively (REQ-REG-064, ADR-REG-017). `subjectKind` includes PACKAGE_DIRECTORY (ADR-REG-018).

No response carries SQL text or a connection setting (REQ-REG-013, AC-REG-014); the frontend never asks for one.

## States

Not applicable — there is no REG screen to hold an empty, loading, error or offline state. The error routing of the three reads (for a consuming screen) is Part B's (frontend-execution-plan-reg.md, F2).

<<<ADR: ADR-REG-001.md>>>
# ADR-REG-001 — RPT owns the Check run record, the findings and the Check Document record; CHK writes through RPT's interface; DOC reads documents but owns no stored rows
Status      : RESOLVED-IN-DIALOGUE
Stage       : P0        Module: REG        Version: v1
Lane        : analysis-dialogue · operator (single-operator converging dialogue)
Decided     : 2026-10-01T12:00:00+00:00
Dialogue-key: 5b4b0d026090
traces      : —

## Decision
Closes project-registry OQ-1 and OQ-2. Owner statement (2026-10-01): the raw idea §14 puts "Runs, findings, documents" under RPT, so RPT owns the Check run record (the raw idea's CHECK_RUN), the findings (CHECK_FINDING) and the Check Document record (CHECK_DOCUMENT). CHK runs the pipeline and writes its results through RPT's interface. DOC fetches and reads documents but does not own their stored rows. The term "Check" stays CHK vocabulary (domain-profile §7.1); ownership of the stored record is RPT's.

Accepted in dialogue: owner input, no counter-proposal. Sources: [KB:raw-idea.md §9, §14]; domain-profile §4 rows 3-4; project-registry §8.

Source      : governance-shared/analysis/modules/REG/_state/briefs/pass-1.md (P0 operator run)

<<<END ADR>>>

<<<ADR: ADR-REG-002.md>>>
# ADR-REG-002 — Keep the owner's dependency graph and carry CHK's writes to RPT through a result port that CHK declares and RPT implements
Status      : RESOLVED-IN-DIALOGUE
Stage       : P0        Module: REG        Version: v1
Lane        : analysis-dialogue · operator (single-operator converging dialogue)
Decided     : 2026-10-01T12:00:00+00:00
Dialogue-key: af0fd36aca95
traces      : —

## Decision
The owner's graph has RPT depends_on [CHK] (agreeing with domain-profile §6), while the OQ-1 answer makes CHK write its results through RPT's interface. A direct CHK -> RPT call would add the edge CHK -> RPT and close a cycle. Options: A) flip the edge (CHK depends_on RPT; RPT depends on nothing but REG) — contradicts both the owner's graph and §6; B) keep the graph and invert the write: CHK declares the port it writes results to, RPT implements it, wired in-process (profile conventions.module_interface: in_process). Recommended B: it honours the owner's graph and §6 unchanged and keeps the graph acyclic; RPT still owns the records (ADR-REG-001).

Reviewer challenge: a port declared by CHK for data RPT owns blurs ownership. Answer: the port carries CHK's results as values; the stored records and their schema stay RPT's. Status: recommended — pending owner confirmation at prd-approval. Sources: domain-profile §6; owner platform-dependency input.

Source      : governance-shared/analysis/modules/REG/_state/briefs/pass-1.md (P0 operator run)

<<<END ADR>>>

<<<ADR: ADR-REG-003.md>>>
# ADR-REG-003 — Service package versions are never changed in place; a new Check uses the current version and every loaded version stays resolvable
Status      : RESOLVED-IN-DIALOGUE
Stage       : P0        Module: REG        Version: v1
Lane        : analysis-dialogue · operator (single-operator converging dialogue)
Decided     : 2026-10-01T12:00:00+00:00
Dialogue-key: 74e8a8e615b1
traces      : POL-REG-005

## Decision
POL-REG-005 and G11 require every report to record the service package version it was built on. A version number is only traceable if the content under it never changes. Recommended: once a version is loaded it is never edited in place — a change is a new version; a new Check uses the current version of its service; every version that was loaded stays resolvable so a stored report's version can be traced back to its service knowledge and service definition. Alternative rejected: record the number only and allow in-place edits — the recorded version would no longer identify what the Check used.

Status: recommended — pending owner confirmation at prd-approval. Sources: [KB:raw-idea.md §4]; domain-profile §5 G11.

Source      : governance-shared/analysis/modules/REG/_state/briefs/pass-1.md (P0 operator run)

<<<END ADR>>>

<<<ADR: ADR-REG-004.md>>>
# ADR-REG-004 — The read-only JDBC data source for blob fetching is a REG Connection of type jdbc
Status      : RESOLVED-IN-DIALOGUE
Stage       : P0        Module: REG        Version: v1
Lane        : analysis-dialogue · operator (single-operator converging dialogue)
Decided     : 2026-10-01T12:00:00+00:00
Dialogue-key: 09f562588a75
traces      : POL-REG-010, POL-REG-012

## Decision
The raw idea §6 reads BLOB columns directly over JDBC with a read-only user, never through MCP (G14), and §4 defines connections once, outside the service packages, set per environment (POL-REG-010, POL-REG-011). Recommended: that JDBC data source is a Connection of type `jdbc` held in REG beside the `mcp` connections, so it is set at activation per environment and bound by the read-only rule (POL-REG-012). Alternative rejected: DOC holds its own data source — a second, unregistered place for host-data credentials.

Status: recommended — pending owner confirmation at prd-approval. Sources: [KB:raw-idea.md §4, §6]; domain-profile §5 G3, G14.

Source      : governance-shared/analysis/modules/REG/_state/briefs/pass-1.md (P0 operator run)

<<<END ADR>>>

<<<ADR: ADR-REG-005.md>>>
# ADR-REG-005 — REG uses the profile's closed fetch-mode enum directly and takes no dependency on DOC
Status      : RESOLVED-IN-DIALOGUE
Stage       : P0        Module: REG        Version: v1
Lane        : analysis-dialogue · operator (single-operator converging dialogue)
Decided     : 2026-10-01T12:00:00+00:00
Dialogue-key: c5400255ad48
traces      : POL-REG-008

## Decision
The Fetch Mode term is DOC vocabulary (domain-profile §7.1), but each service definition carries the value (POL-REG-008) and REG is tier 0 with no dependencies. The profile fixes fetch mode as a closed enum (path | blob | manual) owned by the service (conventions.lookups). Recommended: REG accepts exactly those three values in a service definition from the closed enum, with no runtime read of DOC, so no REG -> DOC edge exists; DOC interprets the value.

Status: recommended — pending owner confirmation at prd-approval. Sources: profile conventions.lookups; [KB:raw-idea.md §6].

Source      : governance-shared/analysis/modules/REG/_state/briefs/pass-1.md (P0 operator run)

<<<END ADR>>>

<<<ADR: ADR-REG-006.md>>>
# ADR-REG-006 — The allowed storage root is DOC's environment setting and the check limits are platform configuration; neither is REG data
Status      : RESOLVED-IN-DIALOGUE
Stage       : P0        Module: REG        Version: v1
Lane        : analysis-dialogue · operator (single-operator converging dialogue)
Decided     : 2026-10-01T12:00:00+00:00
Dialogue-key: cd6176970c86
traces      : —

## Decision
G5 (file paths inside the allowed storage root) and G8 (timeout, maximum rows, maximum file size per Check) need configured values. Recommended: the storage root is an environment setting of DOC, which opens the files; the limits are platform configuration applied to every Check (profile backend phase CORE: "configuration, limits (timeout / rows / file size)"). Neither becomes part of the service definition or a Connection in this version. Alternative: per-service limits in the service definition — no source states a per-service need.

Status: recommended — pending owner confirmation at prd-approval. Sources: [KB:raw-idea.md §12]; domain-profile §5 G5, G8; profile tracks.backend CORE.

Source      : governance-shared/analysis/modules/REG/_state/briefs/pass-1.md (P0 operator run)

<<<END ADR>>>

<<<ADR: ADR-REG-007.md>>>
# ADR-REG-007 — Service packages are files in the administrator's package directory, loaded at start-up; REG exposes read operations only
Status      : ACCEPTED
Stage       : P1        Module: REG        Version: v1
Context     : US-REG-002 asks that a new service goes live without a code change or a release, US-REG-003 that service packages come only from the service administrator, and the scope exceptions exclude a full administration UI and any internal permission system; caller authentication is deferred (raw-idea A2). No upstream text says HOW the service administrator hands a service package to the service. The raw idea §4 shows the package as two files in a folder per service (`services/<service-code>/knowledge.md` + `service.yaml`). An HTTP write operation for packages would be an unauthenticated surface that accepts SQL text while caller authentication is deferred.
Decision    : The service administrator maintains each service package as a folder of the configured package directory (an environment setting outside the deployable, so changing it is neither a code change nor a release), holding the service knowledge file and the service definition file of the raw idea §4. The service registry loads the directory every time the service starts: a new or changed package becomes a new stored version (ADR-REG-003), an identical one changes nothing, an invalid one is rejected and reported, a service whose folder disappeared is marked withdrawn (new Checks are refused; its stored versions stay resolvable). Every outcome of a load run is kept as a Load Result row — the load report — so nothing is skipped silently (raw idea §12). REG exposes no HTTP operation that creates or changes a service package or a connection. Its HTTP surface is read-only: the list of services, one service's current version summary, and the load report; responses never carry SQL text or connection settings. The full content of every version (service knowledge, service definition, queries) is available in-process to CHK, DOC, RPT and INT through the REG interface. Best practice followed: configuration-as-files with an immutable stored history; least exposure while authentication is deferred.
Consequences: P2 stores loaded versions and load results in the service schema. P3.1 designs GET operations only for REG; no POST/PUT/DELETE on REG resources. A restart of the service is how a new package goes live in v1; a hot reload is a later-version option. The administration UI stays out of scope (AIAS-2).
traces      : US-REG-002, US-REG-003, US-REG-004, REQ-REG-005, REQ-REG-007, REQ-REG-010, REQ-REG-013, REQ-REG-017, ENT-REG-006

<<<END ADR>>>

<<<ADR: ADR-REG-008.md>>>
# ADR-REG-008 — REG states every raw-idea §12 guardrail at the surface it controls; the runtime enforcement stays with the module that runs it
Status      : ACCEPTED
Stage       : P1        Module: REG        Version: v1
Context     : Profile check AIAS-1 requires every guardrail of raw-idea §12 to be a REQ with at least one AC, and the owner made the §12 guardrails mandatory requirements of this version (A2). Several guardrails act at Check time in other modules (the LLM call in CHK, file opening in DOC, the approval call in INT), while REG holds only configuration (ADR-REG-001, ADR-REG-006). Writing CHK/DOC/INT behaviour into the REG SRS would be invented scope (no REG story asks for it) and would duplicate the owning module's requirements.
Decision    : Every §12 guardrail gets a REG requirement that constrains what REG accepts or supplies, traced to the REG story it serves: (1) LLM never writes SQL nor triggers approval → queries supplied only through the query channel, apart from the service knowledge (REQ-REG-030) and the approval API definition supplied only to the Employee Decision path (REQ-REG-044); (2) approval only on the employee's action → REQ-REG-044, REQ-REG-045; (3) read-only host access → a connection not declared read-only is refused (REQ-REG-055) and a query that is not a single SELECT statement is rejected (REQ-REG-033); (4) bound parameters, no SQL from free text → only named bind parameters of the declared input (REQ-REG-032); (5) file paths inside the storage root → a package holds no file location, document locations come only from host data through the path column (REQ-REG-041); (6) nothing skipped silently → every rejected package or connection is recorded in the load report (REQ-REG-007); (7) document content is data → the service knowledge is supplied as the only service instruction text, apart from query results and document content (REQ-REG-018); (8) per-Check limits → a service definition carrying its own timeout, row or file-size limit is rejected so the platform limits apply to every Check (REQ-REG-034, ADR-REG-006); (9) nothing carried between Checks → no request data in the registry and package content supplied read-only (REQ-REG-059, REQ-REG-060). The single-SELECT check is defence in depth beside the read-only database user (D2); it does not replace it.
Consequences: The CHK, DOC and INT SRSs carry the Check-time enforcement of guardrails 1, 2, 5, 6, 7 and 8; they read REG's guarantees, they do not re-validate them. P3.1 review (AIAS-3..AIAS-8) scores the enforcement where it runs.
traces      : US-REG-003, US-REG-006, US-REG-007, US-REG-008, US-REG-011, US-REG-013, REQ-REG-007, REQ-REG-018, REQ-REG-030, REQ-REG-032, REQ-REG-033, REQ-REG-034, REQ-REG-041, REQ-REG-044, REQ-REG-055, REQ-REG-059

<<<END ADR>>>

<<<ADR: ADR-REG-009.md>>>
# ADR-REG-009 — Connections come from the environment's activation configuration at start-up; REG stores a credential reference, never a secret
Status      : ACCEPTED
Stage       : P1        Module: REG        Version: v1
Context     : US-REG-009 and US-REG-010 require each connection to be defined once, outside the service packages, and set per environment at activation; US-REG-011 requires a read-only database user. The raw idea §4 shows a connection as `name`, `type`, `endpoint`, `query_tool`, `dialect` and leaves credentials unstated. No upstream text says where the activation configuration lives, whether REG stores it, or how the read-only property is known to the service.
Decision    : Each environment carries an activation configuration (an environment setting, like the package directory of ADR-REG-007) listing its connections. Every start of the service in that environment is its activation: REG registers each listed connection, updates one whose settings changed, and removes one no longer listed — service packages are never touched. A connection holds name, type (`mcp` | `jdbc`), endpoint, query tool (mcp), dialect, environment name, a read-only declaration, a limited-to-views declaration and a credential reference: the name under which the environment's secret store holds the credential. REG never stores the credential itself. The service administrator declares the database user read-only in the activation configuration; a connection without that declaration is refused and reported (raw idea §12). The read-only property itself is enforced at the database user (D2); REG's refusal is the configuration-side guard. Best practice followed: externalised, per-environment configuration with secrets referenced, never persisted.
Consequences: P2 stores connections without any secret column. P3.1 exposes no HTTP operation on connections; CHK and DOC read them in-process. A current service package version whose query names a connection that the environment does not register is refused to Checks until the connection is activated.
traces      : US-REG-009, US-REG-010, US-REG-011, REQ-REG-049, REQ-REG-050, REQ-REG-051, REQ-REG-053, REQ-REG-055, ENT-REG-005

<<<END ADR>>>

<<<ADR: ADR-REG-010.md>>>
# ADR-REG-010 — Closed REG lookups are CHECK constraints; open lookups are data entered by loading the pilot package; no lookup table
Status      : ACCEPTED
Stage       : P2        Module: REG        Version: v1
Context     : SRS A6 declares four closed keys (FETCH_MODE consumed by value, CONNECTION_TYPE, LOAD_OUTCOME, LOAD_SUBJECT) and two open keys (SERVICE_CODE, DOCUMENT_TYPE). The P2 engine asks for seed rows per lookup but forbids columns without SRS origin; the SRS declares no lookup entity, and the open keys' values are fields of ENT-REG-001 and ENT-REG-004, which are filled by loading package folders (ADR-REG-007), never by a client.
Decision    : Each closed key is enforced by a named CHECK constraint on the column it backs, listing every value of A6. No generic lookup table is created (it would be a column invention). The open keys need no seed SQL: SERVICE_CODE `scholarship-request` and DOCUMENT_TYPE `TRANSCRIPT`, `ID_CARD` enter REG_SVC_PKG and REG_REQ_DOC when the delivered pilot package folder is loaded at start-up (REQ-REG-057). Best practice followed: closed domain values as CHECK constraints; configuration-derived values not duplicated as seed rows.
Consequences: The backend plan's bootstrap names the pilot package folder (source: delivered package directory) as the producer of the open values, and the CHECK constraints as the source of the closed ones. Adding a closed value is a migration.
traces      : ENT-REG-001, ENT-REG-002, ENT-REG-004, ENT-REG-005, ENT-REG-006, REQ-REG-036, REQ-REG-052, REQ-REG-057

<<<END ADR>>>

<<<ADR: ADR-REG-011.md>>>
# ADR-REG-011 — The start-up load run and the in-process interface are service-layer procedures, not API blocks; load-time rule messages are load reasons, not HTTP errors
Status      : ACCEPTED
Stage       : P3.1      Module: REG        Version: v1
Context     : ADR-REG-007 gives REG no HTTP write operation: packages and connections are written only by the start-up load run, and CHK, DOC, RPT and INT read REG in-process (CON-REG-007, CON-REG-009, CON-REG-011, CON-REG-012, CON-REG-013). The P3.1 engine models endpoints as `API-*` blocks derived 1:1 into the OpenAPI document, wants every `QR-*` reached by an `API-*` (C7.22), and wants every user-facing RULE message as an error-catalog row answered by an HTTP response (C7.30). Load-time rules (RULE-REG-001 … RULE-REG-015, RULE-REG-018 … RULE-REG-020) never answer an HTTP request: their outcome is a Load Result row. The SRS gives every message in English only, while the profile lists en and ar with `require_all: false`.
Decision    : (1) Only the three HTTP read operations are `API-*` blocks (API-REG-001 … API-REG-003) and appear in `api-spec-reg.yaml`. The load run (activation, package validation, version registration, withdrawal) and the in-process `ServiceRegistry` interface are specified in the SVC-API phase as service-layer procedures, each with its `Honours: CON-…` line, its rule text and its persistence intent written inline against exact tables and `DBF-*` ids. The QR catalog carries the queries reached by the API blocks; the load-run and in-process persistence is not catalogued under `QR-*`. (2) A load-time rule message is stored as the Load Result `REASON` (DBF-REG-047) behind a load reason code `REG-LOAD-{SLUG}` listed in the plan's load reason table; it is not an error-catalog row. The error catalog carries only HTTP errors: RULE-REG-016 → `REG-404-SERVICE-NOT-FOUND`, and `REG-500` as PLATFORM-STD for an unexpected server failure. RULE-REG-017 and the in-process not-found cases are typed exceptions of the in-process interface, also listed in the load reason table's in-process section. (3) Arabic message texts are `PENDING ADR-REG-011` — the SRS states English only and inventing translations is forbidden; they are added when the SRS carries them.
Consequences: The frontend and test stages bind only to the three GET operations. The test stage derives load-run test cases from the ACs through the load report (API-REG-003) and the in-process interface. A later version that adds a hot-reload endpoint turns the load run into an `API-*` block and catalogues its queries.
traces      : REQ-REG-005, REQ-REG-007, REQ-REG-008, REQ-REG-012, REQ-REG-024, REQ-REG-048, REQ-REG-053, RULE-REG-016, RULE-REG-017

<<<END ADR>>>

<<<ADR: ADR-REG-012.md>>>
# ADR-REG-012 — REG's frontend plan has no screen: no SCR, no UXD; the screen-bearing phases carry no SUB and hold only the client contract of the three read operations
Status      : ACCEPTED
Stage       : P3.2      Module: REG        Version: v1
Context     : The P3.2 engine mints one `SCR-*` per SRS screen entry and one `UXD-*` per foreign field a screen of this module renders; its frontend phases F1–F4 are `sub_bearing` (one SUB per screen). The REG SRS declares no screen (PART B "Not applicable", SCR-REQ 0): the full administration UI is out of scope (raw idea §2, AIAS-2) and the employee frontend of amendment A1 is owned by INT, which holds all five screens and their SCR-REQs. Caller authentication and the security phases are deferred (raw idea A2), so the frontend profile has no SEC-FE phase. REG still publishes three read operations (API-REG-001 … API-REG-003 in `api-spec-reg.yaml`) that the employee frontend may consume like any host (A1: "It consumes the same REST API as any host"; REQ-REG-014 rationale: "The employee frontend and hosts see the required documents and fetch mode of a service"). Minting a screen or a flow to give the plan SUBs would invent navigation the SRS does not have (engine A.2, B1/B4). The test stage requires every split unit of the frontend plan to be named by ≥1 TC (C10.11); with no SUB, the units are the phases F1–F4 themselves (ALIGN-FE is `no_tests`).
Decision    : (1) REG v1 mints no `SCR-*` and no `UXD-*`. The flow diagram and the ui-ux-spec state that no REG screen and no REG flow exist, with the reason, and add nothing. (2) The frontend execution plan keeps every profile phase (F1, F2, F3, F4, ALIGN-FE, in order) with its START/END markers and `traces=`, and no SUB in any of them. F1–F3 (the `binds_api` phases) hold only REG's client contract for its three read operations — types read from `api-spec-reg.yaml`, the read queries with their cache keys and error routing, and the response validators — bound by `API-*` id, so that an INT screen that renders REG data reuses one contract instead of re-deriving it; any such field is cited as a `UXD-*` in INT's own plan (the displaying module owns it, engine A.5), never here. F4 states that REG has no screen and no route, and that the frontend holds no affordance that writes a REG resource (REQ-REG-017). The plan's data phase still carries one `screen-hooks` block (the toolkit requires the block in every frontend plan, C0.1): it names the one consuming screen requirement the SRSs state — INT's Document upload, SCR-REQ-INT-003 (REQ-INT-016 reads the required document types with GET /api/v1/services/{serviceCode}) — and binds the reused read to API-REG-002; it sits at phase level, not in a SUB, and mints nothing. (3) The load report read (API-REG-003) is typed and given a read query for completeness of the published contract; no v1 screen renders it (the Service Administrator reads it directly — no administration UI). (4) The P4 frontend test plan derives its cases from the ACs of the three read operations and of REQ-REG-017, each naming one of F1–F4 as its package; no UI flow, no integration case (0 UXD) is fabricated.
Consequences: C9.4, C9.6, C9.7, C9.15, C9.17, C9.22 and C9.24's screen part examine nothing for REG v1 — reported as such in ALIGN-FE, never as ✓. A later version that adds an administration UI mints its SCR/UXD by continuing the sequences from 001. Non-breaking: no REQ, RULE or locked decision is contradicted.
traces      : REQ-REG-008, REQ-REG-013, REQ-REG-014, REQ-REG-015, REQ-REG-017, AC-REG-008, AC-REG-014, AC-REG-015, AC-REG-016, AC-REG-018

<<<END ADR>>>

<<<ADR: ADR-REG-013.md>>>
# ADR-REG-013 — The owner confirmed ADR-REG-002 … ADR-REG-006 and the PRD's recommended decisions at prd-approval on 2026-10-01
Status      : ACCEPTED
Stage       : P0.5      Module: REG        Version: v1
Context     : ADR-REG-002 … ADR-REG-006 and the RESOLVED DECISIONS rows of the P0 artifacts (platform-summary row 4, business-policies rows 2-3, module-registry rows 3-6) and of the PRD (rows 1, 2, 4, 5) were written as "recommended — pending owner confirmation at prd-approval". The gate record `_state/approvals/prd-approval.json` (2026-10-01T10:56:29Z) states: "Hesham Ezzat (owner) — PRD as is + agent decisions 1-9 / ADR-REG-002..006 confirmed 2026-10-01". The confirmation was not written back, so an artifact read in isolation told the reader the opposite of what happened (gate-analysis finding G8). An existing ADR is never rewritten under its id.
Decision    : ADR-REG-002, ADR-REG-003, ADR-REG-004, ADR-REG-005 and ADR-REG-006 are CONFIRMED by the owner at prd-approval 2026-10-01; their "Status: recommended — pending owner confirmation at prd-approval" sentence is superseded by this ADR and their files stay unchanged. The RESOLVED DECISIONS rows of the P0 artifacts and of the PRD now read "confirmed by owner at prd-approval 2026-10-01", and the PRD's Status and APPROVAL block record the approval (Approved by: Hesham Ezzat (owner), Date: 2026-10-01).
Consequences: No decision changes; only its recorded status. A reader of ADR-REG-002 … ADR-REG-006 finds the confirmation here (the ADR index lists this ADR as their confirmation record).
traces      : ADR-REG-002, ADR-REG-003, ADR-REG-004, ADR-REG-005, ADR-REG-006, US-REG-001, US-REG-004, US-REG-007, US-REG-011

<<<END ADR>>>

<<<ADR: ADR-REG-014.md>>>
# ADR-REG-014 — Traces of ADR-REG-001 and ADR-REG-002, which were recorded empty
Status      : ACCEPTED
Stage       : P0        Module: REG        Version: v1
Context     : ADR-REG-001 (RPT owns the Check run record, the findings and the Check Document record) and ADR-REG-002 (CHK writes to RPT through a result port CHK declares and RPT implements) carry "traces: —", although REG artifacts cite them directly: the SRS out-of-scope bullet on Check / Check Document ownership, the contract's "Read by" header, and the ownership split current-business-policies.md and current-prd.md reference (gate-analysis finding G13). An existing ADR is never rewritten under its id, so the traces are recorded here.
Decision    : ADR-REG-001 traces: project-registry OQ-1, project-registry OQ-2 (platform-level, no REG atom owns the decided records); within REG it is applied by the SRS A2 out-of-scope bullet (Check runs, findings and Check Documents are not REG data) and by REQ-REG-059 (no request data in the registry). ADR-REG-002 traces: project-registry OQ-1, domain-profile §6 (RPT depends_on CHK) — platform-level, no REG atom; within REG it is reflected only by SRS A8 `consumes: []` (REG stays tier 0, unaffected by the CHK → RPT result port).
Consequences: Traceability tools and readers resolve ADR-REG-001 and ADR-REG-002 through this ADR. No decision changes.
traces      : ADR-REG-001, ADR-REG-002, REQ-REG-059

<<<END ADR>>>

<<<ADR: ADR-REG-015.md>>>
# ADR-REG-015 — One start-up load run at a time: the whole run is one transaction under one exclusive database lock
Status      : ACCEPTED
Stage       : P1        Module: REG        Version: v1
Context     : The load run (ADR-REG-007) deletes the Load Result rows of the previous run, activates connections (insert / update / delete REG_CONNECTION), registers versions, withdraws or restores services and writes the new Load Result rows. As first planned it ran one transaction per item and named a concurrency guard only for the version insert (UQ_REG_SVC_PKG_VER_PKG_VERSION). Two instances of the service starting together in one environment could interleave the delete / insert of REG_LOAD_RESULT (leaving a mixed or partial load report that QR-REG-005 would misreport) and race on connection update / remove (gate-analysis finding G2). Nothing states a single-instance deployment.
Decision    : The whole load run — Load Result replacement, connection activation, package loading, version registration, withdraw / restore — runs as ONE database transaction that first takes one exclusive lock on the load report table (`LOCK TABLE REG_LOAD_RESULT IN EXCLUSIVE MODE WAIT {timeout}`, oracle19c) and holds it until commit. A package or connection that fails validation rolls back only to the savepoint taken before that item (a failing item never undoes another — REQ-REG-007 unchanged) and is recorded REJECTED. An instance that cannot take the lock within `aias.registry.load-lock-timeout` (default PT30S) runs no load run and serves the registry as stored. An instance that takes the lock after another instance committed runs a full load run against the now-current registry (every unchanged package records UNCHANGED, so the result is idempotent). The unique constraints stay as a backstop. Readers (API-REG-001 … API-REG-003, the in-process interface) never wait: Oracle readers see the last committed state, so a load report is always the complete report of exactly one run. Alternatives rejected: a monotonic LOAD_RUN_ID (needs a new column and still leaves connection activation racing); a lock-row table (a new entity for a purely technical guard); DBMS_LOCK (needs an extra grant).
Consequences: SRS gains REQ-REG-067 and REQ-REG-068; P3.1 states the lock and the savepoints in the load run and the property in CORE; P4 adds a concurrent-start TC and a lock-timeout TC. No schema change.
traces      : REQ-REG-007, REQ-REG-009, REQ-REG-067, REQ-REG-068, ENT-REG-005, ENT-REG-006

<<<END ADR>>>

<<<ADR: ADR-REG-016.md>>>
# ADR-REG-016 — ServiceSummary carries `available`: a read of a withdrawn service says so
Status      : ACCEPTED
Stage       : P1        Module: REG        Version: v1
Context     : RULE-REG-016 refuses a read only for an unknown code, so GET /api/v1/services/{serviceCode} returns a withdrawn service's current version with 200, and ServiceSummary had no field that tells it apart from a live one; the list (API-REG-001) already filters to available services. INT's Document upload screen binds API-REG-002 (gate-analysis finding G3).
Decision    : ServiceSummary gains the required boolean `available` (true when the Service Package is available, false when withdrawn — DBF-REG-003). REQ-REG-013 and REQ-REG-014 return it; the list keeps returning available services only (so every list row has available = true); a read of a withdrawn code returns 200 with available = false. `available` reflects withdrawal only. Whether every connection of the current version is activated is not a summary field: that refusal (RULE-REG-017) belongs to the in-process supply to a Check, and a removed connection is visible in the load report (outcome REMOVED). Alternative rejected: a 404 for a withdrawn read — it would hide stored versions that stay resolvable (ADR-REG-003).
Consequences: Additive response change in v1 (no consumer is built yet): SRS REQ-REG-013, REQ-REG-014, AC-REG-014, AC-REG-015 and new AC-REG-081; contract CON-REG-008, CON-REG-010; api-spec ServiceSummary; P3.1 API-REG-001/002; P3.2 field list; P4 TCs.
traces      : REQ-REG-013, REQ-REG-014, AC-REG-081, ENT-REG-001

<<<END ADR>>>

<<<ADR: ADR-REG-017.md>>>
# ADR-REG-017 — Service-code equality: trimmed, compared case-insensitively, stored in canonical lower case
Status      : ACCEPTED
Stage       : P1        Module: REG        Version: v1
Context     : The SRS never defined when two service codes are the same. RULE-REG-002 and UQ_REG_SVC_PKG_SERVICE_CODE compared raw text, so `scholarship-request`, `Scholarship-Request` and a trailing-space variant would register as three services, defeating POL-REG-001 (one package per service) and POL-REG-013 (codes come only from the registry — a consumer cannot guess the casing) (gate-analysis finding G4). The owner's standing recommendation: codes are trimmed and compared case-insensitively, stored in one canonical case.
Decision    : Every service code — declared by a package folder, or given to a read or an in-process request — is trimmed and compared case-insensitively. The canonical stored form is lower case (the delivered pilot code `scholarship-request` and every cross-module reference to it are already lower case, so no stored or published value changes; an upper-case canonical form would have renamed the pilot code everywhere). After trimming and lower-casing, a declared code must be 1–100 characters of `a-z`, `0-9` and single hyphens between them (`^[a-z0-9]+(-[a-z0-9]+)*$`); otherwise the package is rejected (RULE-REG-022, load reason REG-LOAD-INVALID-SERVICE-CODE). RULE-REG-002 compares canonical forms. The folder name is not required to equal the code (the code is what the definition declares). DB backstop: CHK_REG_SVC_PKG_SERVICE_CODE.
Consequences: SRS REQ-REG-064, REQ-REG-065, RULE-REG-022; P2 CHECK constraint; P3.1 canonicalisation in the load run, API-REG-002 and the in-process interface; P4 TCs.
traces      : REQ-REG-004, REQ-REG-064, REQ-REG-065, RULE-REG-002, RULE-REG-022, ENT-REG-001

<<<END ADR>>>

<<<ADR: ADR-REG-018.md>>>
# ADR-REG-018 — An unreachable or empty package directory fails safe: nothing is withdrawn and the load report says why
Status      : ACCEPTED
Stage       : P1        Module: REG        Version: v1
Context     : REQ-REG-010 withdraws every stored service code that has no folder. A missing, unreadable or empty package directory (a wrong path, a failed volume mount) would withdraw every service at the next start, and every Check would be refused, with no trace distinguishing it from an administrator who removed every folder (gate-analysis finding G5; raw idea §12 "nothing is skipped silently").
Decision    : When the configured package directory does not exist or cannot be read, or exists but holds no package folder while the registry holds at least one available Service Package, the load run loads no package, registers no version and withdraws or restores no service: the registry keeps its previous state and stays usable. It records one Load Result with subject kind PACKAGE_DIRECTORY (new closed LOAD_SUBJECT value), subject name = the configured directory path, outcome REJECTED and the reason of RULE-REG-023 (load reason REG-LOAD-PACKAGE-DIRECTORY-UNAVAILABLE). Connection activation still runs (it reads a different source). Retiring every service remains possible by removing the folders one start at a time or emptying the directory while the registry holds no available service — v1 offers no "withdraw all" act. An empty directory with no available service in the registry is a normal, empty load.
Consequences: SRS REQ-REG-066, RULE-REG-023, LOAD_SUBJECT gains PACKAGE_DIRECTORY; P2 widens CHK_REG_LOAD_RESULT_SUBJECT_KIND; P3.1 guard before package loading; api-spec LoadResult.subjectKind enum; P4 TCs.
traces      : REQ-REG-010, REQ-REG-066, RULE-REG-023, ENT-REG-001, ENT-REG-006

<<<END ADR>>>

<<<ADR: ADR-REG-019.md>>>
# ADR-REG-019 — The platform-wide Check limits are named: aias.check.timeout = PT2M, aias.check.max-rows = 100, aias.check.max-file-size = 10MB
Status      : ACCEPTED
Stage       : P1        Module: REG        Version: v1
Context     : AIAS-7 requires every Check to declare its timeout, maximum rows and maximum file size. ADR-REG-006 (confirmed by ADR-REG-013) makes them platform configuration, never service-definition data (RULE-REG-012), but no REG artifact named the property names or defaults, and the hand-off was untracked (gate-analysis finding G7). CHK's and DOC's backend plans (CORE) already declare the group `aias.check.timeout` (Duration), `aias.check.max-rows` (int), `aias.check.max-file-size` (DataSize); DOC's test plan uses a 10 MB maximum file size and a 100-row boundary.
Decision    : The platform-wide Check limits are the environment properties `aias.check.timeout` (Duration, default PT2M), `aias.check.max-rows` (int, default 100) and `aias.check.max-file-size` (DataSize, default 10MB). They apply to every Check of every service, are owned by the CORE phase of the modules that run Checks (CHK for timeout and rows, DOC for file size and rows on document fetch), and are never read from a service definition (REG RULE-REG-012 rejects `timeout`, `max_rows`, `max_file_size`). REG reads none of them. The hand-off is recorded as a platform finding for CHK's and DOC's own gates to confirm (the project-registry's platform-findings section is maintained by the orchestrator, not by REG).
Consequences: REG SRS A2 out-of-scope bullet, business-policies AUTO row and backend plan CORE cite this ADR and the names; no REG schema or API change.
traces      : REQ-REG-034, RULE-REG-012, US-REG-006

<<<END ADR>>>
